+282|  router.get('/assessments/:id', authMiddleware(db), (req, res) => {
+283|    const isStaff = req.user.role === 'instructor' || req.user.role === 
'teaching_assistant';
+284|    const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = ? 
AND course_id = ?').get(req.params.id, 'BIO-214');
+285|
+286|    if (!assessment) {
+287|      return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Assessment not found' });
+288|    }
+289|
+290|    if (!isStaff && assessment.status !== 'published') {
+291|      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden: Draft assessments 
are staff-only' });
+292|    }
+293|
+294|    const rawItems = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM items WHERE assessment_id = 
? ORDER BY item_order ASC, id ASC').all(assessment.id);
+295|    const rubrics = db.prepare(`
+296|      SELECT rc.* 
+297|      FROM rubric_criteria rc
+298|      JOIN items i ON rc.item_id = i.id
+299|      WHERE i.assessment_id = ?
+300|      ORDER BY rc.criterion_order ASC, rc.id ASC
+301|    `).all(assessment.id);
+302|
+303|    const rubricsByItem = {};
+304|    for (const r of rubrics) {
+305|      if (!rubricsByItem[r.item_id]) {
+306|        rubricsByItem[r.item_id] = [];
+307|      }
+308|      rubricsByItem[r.item_id].push({
+309|        id: r.id,
+310|        label: r.label,
+311|        max_points: r.max_points
+312|      });
+313|    }
+314|
+315|    const items = rawItems.map(it => {
+316|      const itemObj = {
+317|        id: it.id,
+318|        assessment_id: it.assessment_id,
+319|        kind: it.kind,
+320|        prompt: it.prompt,
+321|        options: it.options_json ? JSON.parse(it.options_json) : null,
+322|        points: it.points,
+323|        item_order: it.item_order,
+324|        rubric_criteria: rubricsByItem[it.id] || []
+325|      };
+326|
+327|      // SECURITY & PRIVACY: Only staff get the stored answer key!
+328|      if (isStaff) {
+329|        itemObj.answer = it.answer;
+330|      }
+331|
+332|      return itemObj;
+333|    });
+334|
+335|    const weightRow = db.prepare('SELECT weight_percent FROM 
assessment_weights WHERE course_id = ? AND assessment_id = ?').get('BIO-214', 
assessment.id);
+336|
+337|    res.json({
+338|      assessment: {
+339|        ...assessment,
+340|        weight_percent: weightRow ? weightRow.weight_percent : 0,
+341|        items
+342|      }
+343|    });
+344|  });

+346|  // Create Draft Assessment
+347|  router.post('/assessments', authMiddleware(db), requireInstructor, (req, 
res) => {
+348|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+349|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+350|
+351|    const { title, opens_at, due_at, duration_minutes, max_attempts } = 
req.body || {};
+352|
+353|    if (!title || typeof title !== 'string' || title.trim().length === 0) {
+354|      const err = { error: 'Assessment title is required' };
+355|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+356|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+357|    }
+358|
+359|    if (!isValidIsoDate(opens_at) || !isValidIsoDate(due_at)) {
+360|      const err = { error: 'Opening and due times must be valid parseable 
ISO date strings' };
+361|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+362|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+363|    }
+364|
+365|    if (new Date(opens_at) >= new Date(due_at)) {
+366|      const err = { error: 'Opening time must be strictly before due time' 
};
+367|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+368|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+369|    }
+370|
+371|    const durationNum = Number(duration_minutes);
+372|    if (typeof duration_minutes === 'boolean' || duration_minutes === null 
|| isNaN(durationNum) || !Number.isSafeInteger(durationNum) || durationNum <= 0)
{
+373|      const err = { error: 'Duration must be a positive whole integer 
minute value' };
+374|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+375|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+376|    }
+377|
+378|    const maxAttemptsNum = Number(max_attempts !== undefined ? max_attempts
: 1);
+379|    if (typeof max_attempts === 'boolean' || max_attempts === null || 
isNaN(maxAttemptsNum) || !Number.isSafeInteger(maxAttemptsNum) || maxAttemptsNum
<= 0) {
+380|      const err = { error: 'Max attempts must be a positive whole integer' 
};
+381|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+382|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+383|    }
+384|
+385|    // Generate next ID
+386|    const count = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM 
assessments').get().count;
+387|    const newId = `A-${String(count + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+388|
+389|    const insertStmt = db.prepare(`
+390|      INSERT INTO assessments (id, course_id, title, status, opens_at, 
due_at, duration_minutes, max_attempts)
+391|      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+392|    `);
+393|
+394|    insertStmt.run(
+395|      newId,
+396|      'BIO-214',
+397|      title.trim(),
+398|      'draft',
+399|      new Date(opens_at).toISOString(),
+400|      new Date(due_at).toISOString(),
+401|      durationNum,
+402|      maxAttemptsNum
+403|    );
+404|
+405|    recordAuditEvent(db, {
+406|      timestamp: FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT,
+407|      courseId: 'BIO-214',
+408|      actorId: req.user.id,
+409|      action: 'assessment.created',
+410|      targetType: 'assessment',
+411|      targetId: newId,
+412|      details: `Created draft assessment "${title.trim()}" (${newId})`,
+413|      assessmentId: newId
+414|    });
+415|
+416|    const newRevision = incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+417|
+418|    const created = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = 
?').get(newId);
+419|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 201, { assessment: created, 
course_revision: newRevision }, idCheck);
+420|  });

+422|  // Add Item to Draft Assessment
+423|  router.post('/assessments/:id/items', authMiddleware(db), 
requireInstructor, (req, res) => {
+424|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+425|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+426|
+427|    const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = ? 
AND course_id = ?').get(req.params.id, 'BIO-214');
+428|    if (!assessment) {
+429|      const err = { error: 'Assessment not found' };
+430|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 404, err);
+431|      return res.status(404).json(err);
+432|    }
+433|
+434|    if (assessment.status !== 'draft') {
+435|      const err = { error: 'Cannot add questions to a published assessment'
};
+436|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+437|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+438|    }
+439|
+440|    const { kind, prompt, points, options, answer, rubric_criteria } = 
req.body || {};
+441|
+442|    if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== 'string' || prompt.trim().length === 
0) {
+443|      const err = { error: 'Question prompt is required' };
+444|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+445|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+446|    }
+447|
+448|    const pointsNum = Number(points);
+449|    if (typeof points === 'boolean' || points === null || isNaN(pointsNum) 
|| pointsNum <= 0) {
+450|      const err = { error: 'Points must be a positive numeric value' };
+451|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+452|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+453|    }
+454|
+455|    let parsedOptions = null;
+456|    let storedAnswer = null;
+457|    let parsedCriteria = [];
+458|
+459|    if (kind === 'multiple_choice') {
+460|      if (!Array.isArray(options) || options.length < 2) {
+461|        const err = { error: 'Multiple choice questions require at least 
two distinct options' };
+462|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+463|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+464|      }
+465|
+466|      const trimmedOptions = options.map(o => (typeof o === 'string' ? 
o.trim() : ''));
+467|      if (trimmedOptions.some(o => o.length === 0)) {
+468|        const err = { error: 'All multiple choice options must be nonempty 
strings' };
+469|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+470|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+471|      }
+472|
+473|      const uniqueSet = new Set(trimmedOptions);
+474|      if (uniqueSet.size !== trimmedOptions.length) {
+475|        const err = { error: 'Multiple choice options must be unique after 
trimming' };
+476|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+477|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+478|      }
+479|
+480|      if (!answer || typeof answer !== 'string' || 
!uniqueSet.has(answer.trim())) {
+481|        const err = { error: 'Answer key must match one of the multiple 
choice options' };
+482|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+483|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+484|      }
+485|
+486|      parsedOptions = trimmedOptions;
+487|      storedAnswer = answer.trim();
+488|    } else if (kind === 'written') {
+489|      if (!Array.isArray(rubric_criteria) || rubric_criteria.length === 0) 
{
+490|        const err = { error: 'Written questions require at least one rubric
criterion' };
+491|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+492|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+493|      }
+494|
+495|      for (const rc of rubric_criteria) {
+496|        if (!rc.label || typeof rc.label !== 'string' || 
rc.label.trim().length === 0) {
+497|          const err = { error: 'Every rubric criterion requires a non-empty
label' };
+498|          saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+499|          return res.status(400).json(err);
+500|        }
+501|        const maxPts = Number(rc.max_points);
+502|        if (typeof rc.max_points === 'boolean' || rc.max_points === null ||
isNaN(maxPts) || maxPts <= 0) {
+503|          const err = { error: 'Every rubric criterion requires positive 
max points' };
+504|          saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+505|          return res.status(400).json(err);
+506|        }
+507|        parsedCriteria.push({
+508|          label: rc.label.trim(),
+509|          max_points: maxPts
+510|        });
+511|      }
+512|    } else {
+513|      const err = { error: 'Invalid question kind: must be multiple_choice 
or written' };
+514|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+515|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+516|    }
+517|
+518|    // Insert Item and Rubrics atomically
+519|    const allItemsCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM 
items').get().count;
+520|    const itemId = `I-${String(allItemsCount + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+521|    const itemOrder = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM items WHERE
assessment_id = ?').get(assessment.id).count;
+522|
+523|    const insertTx = db.transaction(() => {
+524|      db.prepare(`
+525|        INSERT INTO items (id, assessment_id, kind, prompt, options_json, 
answer, points, item_order)
+526|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+527|      `).run(
+528|        itemId,
+529|        assessment.id,
+530|        kind,
+531|        prompt.trim(),
+532|        parsedOptions ? JSON.stringify(parsedOptions) : null,
+533|        storedAnswer,
+534|        pointsNum,
+535|        itemOrder
+536|      );
+537|
+538|      if (parsedCriteria.length > 0) {
+539|        const rcCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM 
rubric_criteria').get().count;
+540|        for (let i = 0; i < parsedCriteria.length; i++) {
+541|          const rcId = `RC-${rcCount + 1 + i}`;
+542|          db.prepare(`
+543|            INSERT INTO rubric_criteria (id, item_id, label, max_points, 
criterion_order)
+544|            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+545|          `).run(rcId, itemId, parsedCriteria[i].label, 
parsedCriteria[i].max_points, i);
+546|        }
+547|      }
+548|
+549|      recordAuditEvent(db, {
+550|        timestamp: FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT,
+551|        courseId: 'BIO-214',
+552|        actorId: req.user.id,
+553|        action: 'item.added',
+554|        targetType: 'item',
+555|        targetId: itemId,
+556|        details: `Added ${kind} question (${itemId}) to assessment 
${assessment.id}`,
+557|        assessmentId: assessment.id
+558|      });
+559|
+560|      return incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+561|    });
+562|
+563|    const newRevision = insertTx();
+564|
+565|    const createdItem = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM items WHERE id = 
?').get(itemId);
+566|    const createdRubrics = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM rubric_criteria WHERE 
item_id = ?').all(itemId);
+567|
+568|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 201, {
+569|      item: {
+570|        ...createdItem,
+571|        options: createdItem.options_json ? 
JSON.parse(createdItem.options_json) : null,
+572|        rubric_criteria: createdRubrics
+573|      },
+574|      course_revision: newRevision
+575|    }, idCheck);
+576|  });

+578|  // Publish Draft Assessment
+579|  router.post('/assessments/:id/publish', authMiddleware(db), 
requireInstructor, (req, res) => {
+580|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+581|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+582|
+583|    const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = ? 
AND course_id = ?').get(req.params.id, 'BIO-214');
+584|    if (!assessment) {
+585|      const err = { error: 'Assessment not found' };
+586|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 404, err);
+587|      return res.status(404).json(err);
+588|    }
+589|
+590|    if (assessment.status !== 'draft') {
+591|      const err = { error: 'Assessment is already published' };
+592|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+593|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+594|    }
+595|
+596|    const itemCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM items WHERE
assessment_id = ?').get(assessment.id).count;
+597|    if (itemCount < 1) {
+598|      const err = { error: 'Cannot publish an assessment with zero 
questions' };
+599|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+600|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+601|    }
+602|
+603|    if (assessment.duration_minutes <= 0) {
+604|      const err = { error: 'Cannot publish an assessment without a positive
duration' };
+605|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+606|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+607|    }
+608|
+609|    const publishTx = db.transaction(() => {
+610|      db.prepare("UPDATE assessments SET status = 'published' WHERE id = 
?").run(assessment.id);
+611|
+612|      // Add to assessment_weights at 0% if not present
+613|      db.prepare(`
+614|        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO assessment_weights (course_id, assessment_id,
weight_percent)
+615|        VALUES (?, ?, 0)
+616|      `).run('BIO-214', assessment.id);
+617|
+618|      recordAuditEvent(db, {
+619|        timestamp: FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT,
+620|        courseId: 'BIO-214',
+621|        actorId: req.user.id,
+622|        action: 'assessment.published',
+623|        targetType: 'assessment',
+624|        targetId: assessment.id,
+625|        details: `Published assessment "${assessment.title}" 
(${assessment.id})`,
+626|        assessmentId: assessment.id
+627|      });
+628|
+629|      return incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+630|    });
+631|
+632|    const newRevision = publishTx();
+633|    const updated = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = 
?').get(assessment.id);
+634|
+635|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 200, {
+636|      assessment: updated,
+637|      course_revision: newRevision
+638|    }, idCheck);
+639|  });

+641|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+642|  // Attempts
+643|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+644|  router.get('/attempts', authMiddleware(db), (req, res) => {
+645|    let attempts;
+646|    if (req.user.role === 'instructor') {
+647|      attempts = db.prepare(`
+648|        SELECT att.*, u.name as student_name, u.email as student_email, 
a.title as assessment_title,
+649|               grader.name as assigned_grader_name
+650|        FROM attempts att
+651|        JOIN users u ON att.student_id = u.id
+652|        JOIN assessments a ON att.assessment_id = a.id
+653|        LEFT JOIN users grader ON att.assigned_grader_id = grader.id
+654|        ORDER BY att.started_at DESC, att.id DESC
+655|      `).all();
+656|    } else if (req.user.role === 'teaching_assistant') {
+657|      attempts = db.prepare(`
+658|        SELECT att.*, u.name as student_name, u.email as student_email, 
a.title as assessment_title,
+659|               grader.name as assigned_grader_name
+660|        FROM attempts att
+661|        JOIN users u ON att.student_id = u.id
+662|        JOIN assessments a ON att.assessment_id = a.id
+663|        LEFT JOIN users grader ON att.assigned_grader_id = grader.id
+664|        WHERE att.assigned_grader_id = ?
+665|        ORDER BY att.started_at DESC, att.id DESC
+666|      `).all(req.user.id);
+667|    } else {
+668|      // Student: only their own attempts
+669|      attempts = db.prepare(`
+670|        SELECT att.*, u.name as student_name, u.email as student_email, 
a.title as assessment_title
+671|        FROM attempts att
+672|        JOIN users u ON att.student_id = u.id
+673|        JOIN assessments a ON att.assessment_id = a.id
+674|        WHERE att.student_id = ?
+675|        ORDER BY att.started_at DESC, att.id DESC
+676|      `).all(req.user.id);
+677|
+678|      // PRIVACY FOR STUDENT: If feedback_status !== 'released', hide 
unreleased scores
+679|      attempts = attempts.map(att => {
+680|        // Auto submit if expired
+681|        const updatedAtt = autoSubmitIfExpired(att, req.user);
+682|        if (updatedAtt.feedback_status !== 'released') {
+683|          return {
+684|            ...updatedAtt,
+685|            objective_score: null,
+686|            rubric_score: null,
+687|            total_score: null
+688|          };
+689|        }
+690|        return {
+691|          ...updatedAtt,
+692|          total_score: (updatedAtt.objective_score || 0) + 
(updatedAtt.rubric_score || 0)
+693|        };
+694|      });
+695|    }
+696|
+697|    // If staff, compute total_score and check expired in_progress
+698|    if (req.user.role !== 'student') {
+699|      attempts = attempts.map(att => {
+700|        const updatedAtt = autoSubmitIfExpired(att, req.user);
+701|        const obj = updatedAtt.objective_score;
+702|        const rub = updatedAtt.rubric_score;
+703|        const total = (obj !== null || rub !== null) ? ((obj || 0) + (rub 
|| 0)) : null;
+704|        return {
+705|          ...updatedAtt,
+706|          total_score: total
+707|        };
+708|      });
+709|    }
+710|
+711|    res.json({ attempts });
+712|  });
+713|
+714|  router.get('/attempts/:id', authMiddleware(db), (req, res) => {
+715|    let attempt = db.prepare(`
+716|      SELECT att.*, u.name as student_name, u.email as student_email, 
a.title as assessment_title,
+717|             a.opens_at, a.due_at, a.duration_minutes, grader.name as 
assigned_grader_name
+718|      FROM attempts att
+719|      JOIN users u ON att.student_id = u.id
+720|      JOIN assessments a ON att.assessment_id = a.id
+721|      LEFT JOIN users grader ON att.assigned_grader_id = grader.id
+722|      WHERE att.id = ?
+723|    `).get(req.params.id);
+724|
+725|    if (!attempt) {
+726|      return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Attempt not found' });
+727|    }
+728|
+729|    // Access control
+730|    if (req.user.role === 'student' && attempt.student_id !== req.user.id) 
{
+731|      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden: You can only view 
your own attempts' });
+732|    }
+733|    if (req.user.role === 'teaching_assistant' && 
attempt.assigned_grader_id !== req.user.id) {
+734|      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden: You can only view 
assigned attempts' });
+735|    }
+736|
+737|    // Auto-submit if expired
+738|    attempt = autoSubmitIfExpired(attempt, req.user);
+739|
+740|    const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = 
?').get(attempt.assessment_id);
+741|    const expiryInfo = getAttemptExpiryInfo(attempt, assessment, 
attempt.student_id);
+742|
+743|    const items = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM items WHERE assessment_id = ? 
ORDER BY item_order ASC, id ASC').all(attempt.assessment_id);
+744|    const answers = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM answers WHERE attempt_id = 
?').all(attempt.id);
+745|    const answerMap = {};
+746|    for (const ans of answers) {
+747|      answerMap[ans.item_id] = ans.value;
+748|    }
+749|
+750|    const rubrics = db.prepare(`
+751|      SELECT rc.* 
+752|      FROM rubric_criteria rc
+753|      JOIN items i ON rc.item_id = i.id
+754|      WHERE i.assessment_id = ?
+755|      ORDER BY rc.criterion_order ASC, rc.id ASC
+756|    `).all(attempt.assessment_id);
+757|
+758|    const rubricGrades = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM rubric_grades WHERE 
attempt_id = ?').all(attempt.id);
+759|    const gradesMap = {};
+760|    for (const rg of rubricGrades) {
+761|      gradesMap[rg.criterion_id] = rg;
+762|    }
+763|
+764|    const isStaff = req.user.role === 'instructor' || req.user.role === 
'teaching_assistant';
+765|    const isReleased = attempt.feedback_status === 'released';
+766|
+767|    const rubricsByItem = {};
+768|    for (const r of rubrics) {
+769|      if (!rubricsByItem[r.item_id]) {
+770|        rubricsByItem[r.item_id] = [];
+771|      }
+772|      const grade = gradesMap[r.id];
+773|      const rubricObj = {
+774|        id: r.id,
+775|        label: r.label,
+776|        max_points: r.max_points
+777|      };
+778|
+779|      if (isStaff || isReleased) {
+780|        rubricObj.grade = grade ? {
+781|          score: grade.score,
+782|          feedback: grade.feedback,
+783|          graded_by: grade.graded_by,
+784|          graded_at: grade.graded_at
+785|        } : null;
+786|      } else {
+787|        rubricObj.grade = null;
+788|      }
+789|
+790|      rubricsByItem[r.item_id].push(rubricObj);
+791|    }
+792|
+793|    const itemDetails = items.map(it => {
+794|      const itObj = {
+795|        id: it.id,
+796|        kind: it.kind,
+797|        prompt: it.prompt,
+798|        options: it.options_json ? JSON.parse(it.options_json) : null,
+799|        points: it.points,
+800|        student_answer: answerMap[it.id] !== undefined ? answerMap[it.id] :
null,
+801|        rubric_criteria: rubricsByItem[it.id] || []
+802|      };
+803|
+804|      if (isStaff) {
+805|        itObj.answer = it.answer; // staff gets key
+806|      }
+807|
+808|      return itObj;
+809|    });
+810|
+811|    const responseAttempt = {
+812|      ...attempt,
+813|      expiry_info: expiryInfo,
+814|      items: itemDetails
+815|    };
+816|
+817|    if (!isStaff && !isReleased) {
+818|      responseAttempt.objective_score = null;
+819|      responseAttempt.rubric_score = null;
+820|      responseAttempt.total_score = null;
+821|    } else {
+822|      const obj = responseAttempt.objective_score;
+823|      const rub = responseAttempt.rubric_score;
+824|      responseAttempt.total_score = (obj !== null || rub !== null) ? ((obj 
|| 0) + (rub || 0)) : null;
+825|    }
+826|
+827|    res.json({ attempt: responseAttempt });
+828|  });

+830|  // Start new attempt
+831|  router.post('/attempts/start', authMiddleware(db), (req, res) => {
+832|    if (req.user.role !== 'student') {
+833|      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Only students can start 
attempts' });
+834|    }
+835|
+836|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+837|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+838|
+839|    const { assessment_id } = req.body || {};
+840|    if (!assessment_id) {
+841|      const err = { error: 'assessment_id is required' };
+842|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+843|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+844|    }
+845|
+846|    const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE id = ? 
AND course_id = ?').get(assessment_id, 'BIO-214');
+847|    if (!assessment || assessment.status !== 'published') {
+848|      const err = { error: 'Assessment is not available or not published' 
};
+849|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+850|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+851|    }
+852|
+853|    const acc = db.prepare('SELECT extra_time_minutes, 
deadline_extension_minutes FROM accommodations WHERE course_id = ? AND 
student_id = ?').get('BIO-214', req.user.id);
+854|    const deadlineExt = acc ? acc.deadline_extension_minutes : 0;
+855|    const adjustedDueMs = new Date(assessment.due_at).getTime() + 
(deadlineExt * 60 * 1000);
+856|    const refMomentMs = new Date(FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT).getTime();
+857|    const opensMs = new Date(assessment.opens_at).getTime();
+858|
+859|    if (refMomentMs < opensMs) {
+860|      const err = { error: 'Assessment is not open yet' };
+861|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+862|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+863|    }
+864|
+865|    if (refMomentMs > adjustedDueMs) {
+866|      const err = { error: 'Assessment due date has passed' };
+867|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+868|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+869|    }
+870|
+871|    const existingAttempts = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE 
assessment_id = ? AND student_id = ?').all(assessment.id, req.user.id);
+872|    if (existingAttempts.length >= assessment.max_attempts) {
+873|      const err = { error: 'Attempt limit reached for this assessment' };
+874|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+875|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+876|    }
+877|
+878|    const activeAttempt = existingAttempts.find(a => a.status === 
'in_progress');
+879|    if (activeAttempt) {
+880|      // Check if expired
+881|      const checked = autoSubmitIfExpired(activeAttempt, req.user);
+882|      if (checked.status === 'in_progress') {
+883|        const err = { error: 'You already have an active attempt in 
progress' };
+884|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+885|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+886|      }
+887|    }
+888|
+889|    // Generate Attempt ID
+890|    const count = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM 
attempts').get().count;
+891|    const attemptId = `AT-${100 + count}`;
+892|
+893|    const startTx = db.transaction(() => {
+894|      db.prepare(`
+895|        INSERT INTO attempts (id, assessment_id, student_id, status, 
started_at, assigned_grader_id, feedback_status)
+896|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'in_progress', ?, 'user_2', 'hidden')
+897|      `).run(attemptId, assessment.id, req.user.id, 
FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT);
+898|
+899|      recordAuditEvent(db, {
+900|        timestamp: FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT,
+901|        courseId: 'BIO-214',
+902|        actorId: req.user.id,
+903|        action: 'attempt.started',
+904|        targetType: 'attempt',
+905|        targetId: attemptId,
+906|        details: `Started attempt ${attemptId} for assessment 
"${assessment.title}"`,
+907|        attemptId,
+908|        assessmentId: assessment.id,
+909|        studentId: req.user.id
+910|      });
+911|
+912|      return incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+913|    });
+914|
+915|    const newRevision = startTx();
+916|    const createdAttempt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attemptId);
+917|
+918|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 201, {
+919|      attempt: createdAttempt,
+920|      course_revision: newRevision
+921|    }, idCheck);
+922|  });

+924|  // Save Answers (in progress)
+925|  router.post('/attempts/:id/answers', authMiddleware(db), (req, res) => {
+926|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+927|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+928|
+929|    let attempt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(req.params.id);
+930|    if (!attempt) {
+931|      const err = { error: 'Attempt not found' };
+932|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 404, err);
+933|      return res.status(404).json(err);
+934|    }
+935|
+936|    if (attempt.student_id !== req.user.id) {
+937|      const err = { error: 'Forbidden: You can only save answers to your 
own attempt' };
+938|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 403, err);
+939|      return res.status(403).json(err);
+940|    }
+941|
+942|    // Check expiry
+943|    attempt = autoSubmitIfExpired(attempt, req.user);
+944|    if (attempt.status !== 'in_progress') {
+945|      const err = { error: 'Attempt has ended or expired and cannot accept 
answer writes' };
+946|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+947|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+948|    }
+949|
+950|    const { answers } = req.body || {};
+951|    if (!Array.isArray(answers)) {
+952|      const err = { error: 'answers must be an array of { item_id, value } 
objects' };
+953|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+954|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+955|    }
+956|
+957|    // Validate item IDs belong to assessment
+958|    const validItems = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM items WHERE assessment_id
= ?').all(attempt.assessment_id);
+959|    const validItemIds = new Set(validItems.map(i => i.id));
+960|
+961|    for (const ans of answers) {
+962|      if (!ans || !ans.item_id || !validItemIds.has(ans.item_id)) {
+963|        const err = { error: `Invalid item_id: ${ans ? ans.item_id : 
'missing'} does not belong to this assessment` };
+964|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+965|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+966|      }
+967|    }
+968|
+969|    const saveTx = db.transaction(() => {
+970|      const upsertAnswer = db.prepare(`
+971|        INSERT INTO answers (attempt_id, item_id, value)
+972|        VALUES (?, ?, ?)
+973|        ON CONFLICT(attempt_id, item_id) DO UPDATE SET value = 
excluded.value
+974|      `);
+975|
+976|      for (const ans of answers) {
+977|        upsertAnswer.run(attempt.id, ans.item_id, ans.value !== null && 
ans.value !== undefined ? String(ans.value) : '');
+978|      }
+979|
+980|      return incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+981|    });
+982|
+983|    const newRevision = saveTx();
+984|
+985|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 200, {
+986|      success: true,
+987|      saved_count: answers.length,
+988|      course_revision: newRevision
+989|    }, idCheck);
+990|  });

+994|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+995|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+996|
+997|    let attempt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id =

+998|    if (!attempt) {
+999|      const err = { error: 'Attempt not found' };

+1001|      return res.status(404).json(err);
+1002|    }
+1003|
+1004|    if (attempt.student_id !== req.user.id) {
+1005|      const err = { error: 'Forbidden: You can only submit your own

+1007|      return res.status(403).json(err);

rubricScore, attempt.id);

+1046|        assessmentId: attempt.assessment_id,
+1047|        studentId: req.user.id
+1048|      });
+1049|

+1051|    });
+1052|

+1054|    const updatedAttempt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attempt.id);
+1055|

+1070|  // Grading Endpoints
+1071|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+1072|  router.post('/grading/rubric', authMiddleware(db), requireStaff, (req, 
res) => {
+1073|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+1074|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+1075|
+1076|    const { attempt_id, criterion_id, score, feedback } = req.body || {};
+1077|
+1078|    const attempt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attempt_id);
+1079|    if (!attempt) {
+1080|      const err = { error: 'Attempt not found' };
+1081|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 404, err);
+1082|      return res.status(404).json(err);
+1083|    }
+1084|
+1085|    // Role authorization
+1086|    if (req.user.role === 'teaching_assistant' && 
attempt.assigned_grader_id !== req.user.id) {
+1087|      const err = { error: 'Forbidden: TA can only grade assigned 
attempts' };
+1088|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 403, err);
+1089|      return res.status(403).json(err);
+1090|    }
+1091|
+1092|    if (attempt.status === 'in_progress') {
+1093|      const err = { error: 'Cannot grade an attempt that is still in 
progress' };
+1094|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1095|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1096|    }
+1097|
+1098|    if (attempt.feedback_status === 'released') {
+1099|      const err = { error: 'Cannot modify grades for an already released 
attempt' };
+1100|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1101|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1102|    }
+1103|
+1104|    // Check criterion
+1105|    const criterion = db.prepare(`
+1106|      SELECT rc.*, i.assessment_id
+1107|      FROM rubric_criteria rc
+1108|      JOIN items i ON rc.item_id = i.id
+1109|      WHERE rc.id = ?
+1110|    `).get(criterion_id);
+1111|
+1112|    if (!criterion || criterion.assessment_id !== attempt.assessment_id) {
+1113|      const err = { error: 'Rubric criterion not found or does not belong 
to this assessment' };
+1114|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1115|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1116|    }
+1117|
+1118|    const scoreNum = Number(score);
+1119|    if (typeof score === 'boolean' || score === null || isNaN(scoreNum) ||
scoreNum < 0 || scoreNum > criterion.max_points) {
+1120|      const err = { error: `Score must be a number between 0 and 
${criterion.max_points}` };
+1121|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1122|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1123|    }
+1124|
+1125|    const gradeTx = db.transaction(() => {
+1126|      // Upsert rubric grade
+1127|      db.prepare(`
+1128|        INSERT INTO rubric_grades (attempt_id, criterion_id, score, 
feedback, graded_by, graded_at)
+1129|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+1130|        ON CONFLICT(attempt_id, criterion_id) DO UPDATE SET
+1131|          score = excluded.score,
+1132|          feedback = excluded.feedback,
+1133|          graded_by = excluded.graded_by,
+1134|          graded_at = excluded.graded_at
+1135|      `).run(
+1136|        attempt.id,
+1137|        criterion.id,
+1138|        scoreNum,
+1139|        feedback !== undefined && feedback !== null ? 
String(feedback).trim() : null,
+1140|        req.user.id,
+1141|        FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT
+1142|      );
+1143|
+1144|      // Check all criteria for assessment
+1145|      const allCriteria = db.prepare(`
+1146|        SELECT rc.id
+1147|        FROM rubric_criteria rc
+1148|        JOIN items i ON rc.item_id = i.id
+1149|        WHERE i.assessment_id = ?
+1150|      `).all(attempt.assessment_id);
+1151|
+1152|      const allGrades = db.prepare('SELECT score FROM rubric_grades WHERE 
attempt_id = ?').all(attempt.id);
+1153|      const isComplete = allCriteria.length > 0 && allGrades.length >= 
allCriteria.length;
+1154|      const totalRubricScore = allGrades.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.score,
0);
+1155|
+1156|      const newStatus = isComplete ? 'graded' : 'submitted';
+1157|
+1158|      db.prepare(`
+1159|        UPDATE attempts
+1160|        SET status = ?, rubric_score = ?
+1161|        WHERE id = ?
+1162|      `).run(newStatus, totalRubricScore, attempt.id);
+1163|
+1164|      recordAuditEvent(db, {
+1165|        timestamp: FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT,
+1166|        courseId: 'BIO-214',
+1167|        actorId: req.user.id,
+1168|        action: 'grade.saved',
+1169|        targetType: 'rubric_grade',
+1170|        targetId: criterion.id,
+1171|        details: `Saved rubric grade on attempt ${attempt.id}`,
+1172|        attemptId: attempt.id,
+1173|        assessmentId: attempt.assessment_id,
+1174|        studentId: attempt.student_id
+1175|      });
+1176|
+1177|      return incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+1178|    });
+1179|
+1180|    const newRevision = gradeTx();
+1181|    const updatedAttempt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attempt.id);
+1182|
+1183|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 200, {
+1184|      attempt: updatedAttempt,
+1185|      course_revision: newRevision
+1186|    }, idCheck);
+1187|  });
+1188|
+1189|  // Atomic Grading Worksheet
+1190|  router.post('/grading/worksheet', authMiddleware(db), requireStaff, 
(req, res) => {
+1191|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+1192|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+1193|
+1194|    const { rows } = req.body || {};
+1195|    if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) {
+1196|      const err = { error: 'Worksheet requires a non-empty array of rows' 
};
+1197|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1198|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1199|    }
+1200|
+1201|    // Check for duplicate rows
+1202|    const seenPairs = new Set();
+1203|    for (const r of rows) {
+1204|      if (!r || !r.attempt_id || !r.criterion_id) {
+1205|        const err = { error: 'Every worksheet row must have attempt_id and
criterion_id' };
+1206|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1207|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1208|      }
+1209|      const pairKey = `${r.attempt_id}_${r.criterion_id}`;
+1210|      if (seenPairs.has(pairKey)) {
+1211|        const err = { error: `Duplicate row for attempt ${r.attempt_id} 
and criterion ${r.criterion_id}` };
+1212|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1213|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1214|      }
+1215|      seenPairs.add(pairKey);
+1216|    }
+1217|
+1218|    // Pre-validate all attempts and criteria
+1219|    const attemptIds = Array.from(new Set(rows.map(r => r.attempt_id)));
+1220|    const attemptsMap = {};
+1221|    for (const attId of attemptIds) {
+1222|      const att = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attId);
+1223|      if (!att) {
+1224|        const err = { error: `Attempt ${attId} not found` };
+1225|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 404, err);
+1226|        return res.status(404).json(err);
+1227|      }
+1228|      if (req.user.role === 'teaching_assistant' && att.assigned_grader_id
!== req.user.id) {
+1229|        const err = { error: `Forbidden: TA cannot grade attempt ${attId}`
};
+1230|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 403, err);
+1231|        return res.status(403).json(err);
+1232|      }
+1233|      if (att.status === 'in_progress') {
+1234|        const err = { error: `Cannot grade attempt ${attId} while in 
progress` };
+1235|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1236|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1237|      }
+1238|      if (att.feedback_status === 'released') {
+1239|        const err = { error: `Attempt ${attId} is already released and 
immutable` };
+1240|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1241|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1242|      }
+1243|      attemptsMap[attId] = att;
+1244|    }
+1245|
+1246|    // Pre-validate criteria & scores
+1247|    const validatedRows = [];
+1248|    for (const r of rows) {
+1249|      const att = attemptsMap[r.attempt_id];
+1250|      const criterion = db.prepare(`
+1251|        SELECT rc.*, i.assessment_id
+1252|        FROM rubric_criteria rc
+1253|        JOIN items i ON rc.item_id = i.id
+1254|        WHERE rc.id = ?
+1255|      `).get(r.criterion_id);
+1256|
+1257|      if (!criterion || criterion.assessment_id !== att.assessment_id) {
+1258|        const err = { error: `Criterion ${r.criterion_id} does not belong 
to assessment for attempt ${att.id}` };
+1259|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1260|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1261|      }
+1262|
+1263|      const scoreNum = Number(r.score);
+1264|      if (typeof r.score === 'boolean' || r.score === null || 
isNaN(scoreNum) || scoreNum < 0 || scoreNum > criterion.max_points) {
+1265|        const err = { error: `Invalid score ${r.score} for criterion 
${r.criterion_id}: must be between 0 and ${criterion.max_points}` };
+1266|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1267|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1268|      }
+1269|
+1270|      validatedRows.push({
+1271|        attempt_id: r.attempt_id,
+1272|        criterion_id: r.criterion_id,
+1273|        score: scoreNum,
+1274|        feedback: r.feedback !== undefined && r.feedback !== null ? 
String(r.feedback).trim() : null
+1275|      });
+1276|    }
+1277|
+1278|    const worksheetTx = db.transaction(() => {
+1279|      const upsertGrade = db.prepare(`
+1280|        INSERT INTO rubric_grades (attempt_id, criterion_id, score, 
feedback, graded_by, graded_at)
+1281|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+1282|        ON CONFLICT(attempt_id, criterion_id) DO UPDATE SET
+1283|          score = excluded.score,
+1284|          feedback = excluded.feedback,
+1285|          graded_by = excluded.graded_by,
+1286|          graded_at = excluded.graded_at
+1287|      `);
+1288|
+1289|      for (const vr of validatedRows) {
+1290|        upsertGrade.run(vr.attempt_id, vr.criterion_id, vr.score, 
vr.feedback, req.user.id, FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT);
+1291|      }
+1292|
+1293|      // Update each distinct attempt status & scores
+1294|      for (const attId of attemptIds) {
+1295|        const att = attemptsMap[attId];
+1296|        const allCriteria = db.prepare(`
+1297|          SELECT rc.id
+1298|          FROM rubric_criteria rc
+1299|          JOIN items i ON rc.item_id = i.id
+1300|          WHERE i.assessment_id = ?
+1301|        `).all(att.assessment_id);
+1302|
+1303|        const allGrades = db.prepare('SELECT score FROM rubric_grades 
WHERE attempt_id = ?').all(attId);
+1304|        const isComplete = allCriteria.length > 0 && allGrades.length >= 
allCriteria.length;
+1305|        const totalRubricScore = allGrades.reduce((sum, g) => sum + 
g.score, 0);
+1306|        const newStatus = isComplete ? 'graded' : 'submitted';
+1307|
+1308|        db.prepare(`
+1309|          UPDATE attempts
+1310|          SET status = ?, rubric_score = ?
+1311|          WHERE id = ?
+1312|        `).run(newStatus, totalRubricScore, attId);
+1313|
+1314|        recordAuditEvent(db, {
+1315|          timestamp: FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT,
+1316|          courseId: 'BIO-214',
+1317|          actorId: req.user.id,
+1318|          action: 'grade.saved',
+1319|          targetType: 'attempt',
+1320|          targetId: attId,
+1321|          details: `Saved worksheet grading for attempt ${attId}`,
+1322|          attemptId: attId,
+1323|          assessmentId: att.assessment_id,
+1324|          studentId: att.student_id
+1325|        });
+1326|      }
+1327|
+1328|      // Exactly ONE revision increment for the entire accepted worksheet
+1329|      return incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+1330|    });
+1331|
+1332|    const newRevision = worksheetTx();
+1333|
+1334|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 200, {
+1335|      success: true,
+1336|      saved_rows_count: validatedRows.length,
+1337|      affected_attempts_count: attemptIds.length,
+1338|      course_revision: newRevision
+1339|    }, idCheck);
+1340|  });

+1398|  // Batch Release: Step 1 Preview
+1399|  router.post('/release/preview', authMiddleware(db), requireInstructor, 
(req, res) => {
+1400|    const { attempt_ids } = req.body || {};
+1401|    if (!Array.isArray(attempt_ids) || attempt_ids.length === 0) {
+1402|      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'attempt_ids must be a 
non-empty array' });
+1403|    }
+1404|
+1405|    const uniqueIds = Array.from(new Set(attempt_ids));
+1406|    if (uniqueIds.length !== attempt_ids.length) {
+1407|      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Duplicate attempt IDs in 
selection' });
+1408|    }
+1409|
+1410|    const currentRevision = getCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+1411|    const itemsPreview = [];
+1412|
+1413|    for (const attId of uniqueIds) {
+1414|      const attempt = db.prepare(`
+1415|        SELECT att.*, u.name as student_name, u.email as student_email, 
a.title as assessment_title
+1416|        FROM attempts att
+1417|        JOIN users u ON att.student_id = u.id
+1418|        JOIN assessments a ON att.assessment_id = a.id
+1419|        WHERE att.id = ? AND a.course_id = 'BIO-214'
+1420|      `).get(attId);
+1421|
+1422|      if (!attempt) {
+1423|        return res.status(404).json({ error: `Attempt ${attId} not found 
in this course` });
+1424|      }
+1425|
+1426|      if (attempt.status !== 'graded') {
+1427|        return res.status(400).json({ error: `Attempt ${attId} is not 
fully graded` });
+1428|      }
+1429|
+1430|      if (attempt.feedback_status === 'released') {
+1431|        return res.status(400).json({ error: `Attempt ${attId} is already 
released` });
+1432|      }
+1433|
+1434|      // Calculate max score
+1435|      const maxPts = db.prepare('SELECT SUM(points) as total FROM items 
WHERE assessment_id = ?').get(attempt.assessment_id).total || 0;
+1436|      const totalScore = (attempt.objective_score || 0) + 
(attempt.rubric_score || 0);
+1437|
+1438|      itemsPreview.push({
+1439|        attempt_id: attempt.id,
+1440|        student_id: attempt.student_id,
+1441|        student_name: attempt.student_name,
+1442|        student_email: attempt.student_email,
+1443|        assessment_id: attempt.assessment_id,
+1444|        assessment_title: attempt.assessment_title,
+1445|        objective_score: attempt.objective_score,
+1446|        rubric_score: attempt.rubric_score,
+1447|        total_score: totalScore,
+1448|        max_score: maxPts
+1449|      });
+1450|    }
+1451|
+1452|    const previewId = 'prev_' + crypto.randomUUID();
+1453|    db.prepare(`
+1454|      INSERT INTO batch_release_previews (id, course_id, instructor_id, 
created_revision, attempt_ids_json, created_at, consumed)
+1455|      VALUES (?, 'BIO-214', ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
+1456|    `).run(
+1457|      previewId,
+1458|      req.user.id,
+1459|      currentRevision,
+1460|      JSON.stringify(uniqueIds),
+1461|      FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT
+1462|    );
+1463|
+1464|    return res.json({
+1465|      preview_id: previewId,
+1466|      created_revision: currentRevision,
+1467|      items: itemsPreview
+1468|    });
+1469|  });

+1471|  // Batch Release: Step 2 Commit
+1472|  router.post('/release/commit', authMiddleware(db), requireInstructor, 
(req, res) => {
+1473|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+1474|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+1475|
+1476|    const { preview_id } = req.body || {};
+1477|    if (!preview_id || typeof preview_id !== 'string') {
+1478|      const err = { error: 'preview_id is required' };
+1479|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1480|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1481|    }
+1482|
+1483|    const preview = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM batch_release_previews WHERE
id = ?').get(preview_id);
+1484|    if (!preview) {
+1485|      const err = { error: 'Preview not found' };
+1486|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 404, err);
+1487|      return res.status(404).json(err);
+1488|    }
+1489|
+1490|    if (preview.instructor_id !== req.user.id) {
+1491|      const err = { error: 'Preview belongs to another instructor' };
+1492|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 403, err);
+1493|      return res.status(403).json(err);
+1494|    }
+1495|
+1496|    if (preview.consumed === 1) {
+1497|      const err = { error: 'This release preview has already been 
committed and cannot be reused' };
+1498|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1499|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1500|    }
+1501|
+1502|    // Check if course revision changed since preview was created
+1503|    const currentRevision = getCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+1504|    if (currentRevision !== preview.created_revision) {
+1505|      const err = {
+1506|        error: 'Preview is stale: course revision changed since preview 
was generated. Please create a new preview.',
+1507|        preview_revision: preview.created_revision,
+1508|        current_revision: currentRevision
+1509|      };
+1510|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 409, err);
+1511|      return res.status(409).json(err);
+1512|    }
+1513|
+1514|    const attemptIds = JSON.parse(preview.attempt_ids_json);
+1515|
+1516|    const commitTx = db.transaction(() => {
+1517|      // Mark preview consumed
+1518|      db.prepare('UPDATE batch_release_previews SET consumed = 1 WHERE id 
= ?').run(preview.id);
+1519|
+1520|      const releaseStmt = db.prepare("UPDATE attempts SET feedback_status 
= 'released' WHERE id = ?");
+1521|
+1522|      for (const attId of attemptIds) {
+1523|        const attempt = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attId);
+1524|        releaseStmt.run(attId);
+1525|
+1526|        recordAuditEvent(db, {
+1527|          timestamp: FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT,
+1528|          courseId: 'BIO-214',
+1529|          actorId: req.user.id,
+1530|          action: 'attempt.released',
+1531|          targetType: 'attempt',
+1532|          targetId: attId,
+1533|          details: `Batch released attempt ${attId}`,
+1534|          attemptId: attId,
+1535|          assessmentId: attempt ? attempt.assessment_id : null,
+1536|          studentId: attempt ? attempt.student_id : null
+1537|        });
+1538|      }
+1539|
+1540|      return incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+1541|    });
+1542|
+1543|    const newRevision = commitTx();
+1544|
+1545|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 200, {
+1546|      success: true,
+1547|      released_count: attemptIds.length,
+1548|      released_attempt_ids: attemptIds,
+1549|      course_revision: newRevision
+1550|    }, idCheck);
+1551|  });

+1571|  // Policy update
+1572|  router.post('/policy', authMiddleware(db), requireInstructor, (req, res)
=> {
+1573|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+1574|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+1575|
+1576|    const { weights } = req.body || {};
+1577|    if (!weights || typeof weights !== 'object' || Array.isArray(weights))
{
+1578|      const err = { error: 'weights must be an object mapping published 
assessment IDs to percentage weights' };
+1579|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1580|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1581|    }
+1582|
+1583|    const published = db.prepare("SELECT id FROM assessments WHERE 
course_id = 'BIO-214' AND status = 'published'").all();
+1584|    const publishedIds = new Set(published.map(a => a.id));
+1585|
+1586|    const weightKeys = Object.keys(weights);
+1587|    if (weightKeys.length !== publishedIds.size) {
+1588|      const err = { error: `Policy must include all ${publishedIds.size} 
published assessments exactly once` };
+1589|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1590|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1591|    }
+1592|
+1593|    let sum = 0;
+1594|    const parsedWeights = {};
+1595|
+1596|    for (const id of publishedIds) {
+1597|      if (weights[id] === undefined) {
+1598|        const err = { error: `Missing weight for published assessment 
${id}` };
+1599|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1600|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1601|      }
+1602|
+1603|      const val = weights[id];
+1604|      const num = Number(val);
+1605|      if (typeof val === 'boolean' || val === null || isNaN(num) || num < 
0 || num > 100) {
+1606|        const err = { error: `Invalid weight for ${id}: must be a 
non-negative number <= 100` };
+1607|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1608|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1609|      }
+1610|
+1611|      // Check at most 2 decimal places
+1612|      const strVal = String(num);
+1613|      const dotIndex = strVal.indexOf('.');
+1614|      if (dotIndex !== -1 && strVal.length - dotIndex - 1 > 2) {
+1615|        const err = { error: `Weight for ${id} cannot have more than 2 
decimal places` };
+1616|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1617|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1618|      }
+1619|
+1620|      sum += num;
+1621|      parsedWeights[id] = num;
+1622|    }
+1623|
+1624|    // Check unknown keys
+1625|    for (const k of weightKeys) {
+1626|      if (!publishedIds.has(k)) {
+1627|        const err = { error: `Unknown or draft assessment ID in weights: 
${k}` };
+1628|        saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1629|        return res.status(400).json(err);
+1630|      }
+1631|    }
+1632|
+1633|    if (Math.abs(sum - 100) > 0.0001) {
+1634|      const err = { error: `Total percentage must equal exactly 100% 
(current sum: ${sum.toFixed(2)}%)` };
+1635|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 400, err);
+1636|      return res.status(400).json(err);
+1637|    }
+1638|
+1639|    const policyTx = db.transaction(() => {
+1640|      const upsertWeight = db.prepare(`
+1641|        INSERT INTO assessment_weights (course_id, assessment_id, 
weight_percent)
+1642|        VALUES ('BIO-214', ?, ?)
+1643|        ON CONFLICT(course_id, assessment_id) DO UPDATE SET weight_percent
= excluded.weight_percent
+1644|      `);
+1645|
+1646|      for (const [aId, wt] of Object.entries(parsedWeights)) {
+1647|        upsertWeight.run(aId, wt);
+1648|      }
+1649|
+1650|      recordAuditEvent(db, {
+1651|        timestamp: FIXED_REFERENCE_MOMENT,
+1652|        courseId: 'BIO-214',
+1653|        actorId: req.user.id,
+1654|        action: 'policy.updated',
+1655|        targetType: 'course',
+1656|        targetId: 'BIO-214',
+1657|        details: `Updated assessment outcome weighting policy 
(${JSON.stringify(parsedWeights)})`
+1658|      });
+1659|
+1660|      return incrementCourseRevision(db, 'BIO-214');
+1661|    });
+1662|
+1663|    const newRevision = policyTx();
+1664|
+1665|    return sendAndRecord(db, req, res, 200, {
+1666|      weights: parsedWeights,
+1667|      course_revision: newRevision
+1668|    }, idCheck);
+1669|  });
+1670|
+1671|  // Student Exception update
+1672|  router.post('/exceptions', authMiddleware(db), requireInstructor, (req, 
res) => {
+1673|    const idCheck = checkIdempotencyAndRevision(db, req, res);
+1674|    if (idCheck.error || idCheck.replay) return;
+1675|
+1676|    const { student_id, assessment_id, excused, reason } = req.body || {};
+1677|
+1678|    const enrollment = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM enrollments WHERE 
course_id = 'BIO-214' AND user_id = ? AND kind = 'student'").get(student_id);
+1679|    if (!enrollment) {
+1680|      const err = { error: 'Student not enrolled in course' };
+1681|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 404, err);
+1682|      return res.status(404).json(err);
+1683|    }
+1684|
+1685|    const assessment = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM assessments WHERE 
course_id = 'BIO-214' AND id = ? AND status = 'published'").get(assessment_id);
+1686|    if (!assessment) {
+1687|      const err = { error: 'Assessment not found or not published' };
+1688|      saveReceipt(db, req.user.id, idCheck.operationId, req.method, 
req.path, idCheck.inputHash, 404, err);
+1689|      return res.status(404).json(err);
+1690|    }
+1691|
+1692|    const isExcused = !!excused;

+1704|          ON CONFLICT(course_id, student_id, assessment_id) DO UPDATE SET

+1707|            updated_at = excluded.updated_at

+1724|          ? `Excused assessment ${assessment_id} for student

+1725|          : `Restored assessment ${assessment_id} for student

+1732|

where the magic happens – I'll be using Express to handle the routing and setup.

from the `/app/public/` directory, serving the static files from the same 
directory, mounting the `/api` endpoints, and finally, getting the server to 
listen on port 3000 (and bind to `0.0.0.0` for all interfaces).

Now, let's make sure the server meets the requirements. 

First, I need to make sure the server can be started using `node /app/server.js`
and that it runs on port 3000. It must also serve `index.html` from the 
appropriate directory and persist data to `/app/coursemark.db`. I also need to 
provide a public `GET /api/health` endpoint on the same origin. It needs to 
return a 200 HTTP status code with a non-empty JSON health object. No sign-in or
protected course records are allowed for this endpoint.

Finally, I need to make sure the server runs in a non-watch mode on 
`0.0.0.0:3000`, and that it honors the `DB_PATH` environment variable when it's 
supplied, otherwise using the default path of `/app/coursemark.db`. Okay, let's 
get to writing `/app/server.js`.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"

  file_text: 
    const express = require('express');
    const path = require('path');
    const { initializeDatabase, DB_PATH } = require('./src/db');
    const { createRouter } = require('./src/routes');
    
    const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;
    const HOST = '0.0.0.0';
    
    // Initialize DB and Seed
    const db = initializeDatabase();
    
    const app = express();
    
    // Disable x-powered-by
    app.disable('x-powered-by');
    
    // Middleware
    app.use(express.json());
    app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));
    
    // Serve static assets from public/
    app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'public')));
    
    // API Routes
    app.use('/api', createRouter(db));
    
    // SPA fallback for HTML5 history navigation if any
    app.get('*', (req, res, next) => {
      if (req.path.startsWith('/api')) {
        return next();
      }
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });
    
    // Error handling middleware
    app.use((err, req, res, next) => {
      console.error('Server error:', err);
      res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' });
    });
    
    if (require.main === module) {
      app.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
        console.log(`Coursemark server running on http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
        console.log(`Database persisting to: ${DB_PATH}`);
      });
    }
    
    module.exports = { app, db };

Summary: Create backend test suite in /app/test/backend_test.js

Reasoning:
**My Backend Testing Strategy**

Okay, here's the plan. Before we even think about the frontend, I need to 
thoroughly vet the backend API. I'm going to kick things off by starting the 
server and immediately hitting it with a barrage of tests to endpoints like 
`/health`, `/auth`, `/me`, and `/courses`. The goal is to build a robust and 
reliable foundation. I'll be writing a comprehensive end-to-end test suite in 
`/app/test/e2e.test.js` to cover every API requirement meticulously.

Here’s the test plan I'll be implementing:

1.  **Public Health Check:** Ensure `/api/health` returns a 200 OK without any 
authentication needed. That's our sanity check.

2.  **Authentication:** This is critical. I'll be testing:
    *   Login with an incorrect password should correctly return a 401.
    *   Login with valid credentials for seed users (Ada Mensah, Luis Ortega, 
Nora Kim, Ben Okafor) to cover instructor, teaching assistant and student roles.
    *   Verify Bearer token authentication works as expected on protected 
endpoints.
    *   Accessing a protected endpoint without a token should result in a 401.
    *   Logout should revoke all tokens for the user, and subsequent access 
attempts with the old token should also result in a 401.

3.  **Revision & Idempotency:** This is where we ensure data integrity and 
prevent issues. I'll be testing:
    *   Invalid revision inputs (fractions, strings, booleans) should return a 
400.
    *   Stale revisions (mismatched `expected_revision`) should return a 409.
    *   Replaying the same `operation_id` with the same inputs should return the
original response without incrementing the revision.
    *   Replaying the same `operation_id` with different inputs should return a 
409.
    *   Replaying the same `operation_id` by a different user should also return
a 409.

4.  **Authoring (Instructor):** I will test authoring workflows for instructors 
only, including:
    *   Creating a draft assessment, specifying title, opening and due dates, 
duration, and attempts limit.
    *   Validation: ensuring the system rejects invalid due/opens dates.
    *   Adding assessment items, including multiple-choice with distinct options
(ensuring only one option matches the answer) and written items with rubric 
criteria.
    *   Validation: ensuring the system rejects duplicate assessment options.
    *   Publishing an assessment, ensuring it has at least one item, a positive 
duration, and a weight of 0%.
    *   The system should prevent adding items to published assessments.

5.  **Timed Attempts:** This is all about the student experience.
    *   Verify that a student can start an attempt only within the open window, 
with accommodation applied, and within the max attempts.
    *   Validate expiry calculation to ensure the system auto-submits timed 
attempts after their specified duration.
    *   In-progress answers save correctly.
    *   Manual submission accurately grades multiple choice and updates the 
attempt status.
    *   Students can't see anything, *especially* scores or feedback, until the 
assessment is released. The system needs to respect student privacy.

6.  **Grading:** Instructors and assigned TAs are the focus here.
    *   Verify the system supports single row grading by instructors or assigned
TAs.
    *   Ensure atomic worksheet grading with multiple criteria is working.
    *   Validation: reject worksheets with duplicate or invalid criteria.
    *   Ensure an attempt status changes to 'graded' when all rubric criteria 
are graded.
    *   TA's cannot grade unassigned attempts, and students cannot grade. Both 
scenarios must return a 403.

7.  **Batch Release:** We'll be testing controlled, previewed release.
    *   Previewing a batch release for graded, unreleased attempts.
    *   Committing the batch release using the preview handle.
    *   Handling stale previews (course revision changed) with a 409 error.
    *   After release, students should be able to view their scores and rubric 
feedback.
    *   Students cannot see other students' scores.
    *   Released attempts should be immutable.

8.  **Outcome Ledger:** Calculating outcomes and weighting is crucial.
    *   Policy weights should update correctly based on all published 
assessments, summing to 100%.
    *   Excusing an assessment should remove its weight from the denominator.
    *   Verify the weighted outcome calculation through testing.
    *   Restoring

    const assert = require('assert');
    const http = require('http');
    const path = require('path');
    const fs = require('fs');
    
    // We will test using an in-memory or temporary database to avoid polluting 
the default one,
    // and also test against the app.
    const testDbPath = path.join(__dirname, 'test_coursemark.db');
    if (fs.existsSync(testDbPath)) {
      fs.unlinkSync(testDbPath);
    }
    process.env.DB_PATH = testDbPath;
    
    const { initializeDatabase } = require('../src/db');
    const { app } = require('../server');
    
    let server;
    let baseUrl;
    
    function request(method, path, options = {}) {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const url = new URL(path, baseUrl);
        const headers = options.headers || {};
        let body = options.body;
    
        if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
          body = JSON.stringify(body);
          headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
        }
    
        const req = http.request(url, {
          method: method.toUpperCase(),
          headers
        }, (res) => {
          let data = '';
          res.on('data', chunk => { data += chunk; });
          res.on('end', () => {
            let json = null;
            try {
              json = JSON.parse(data);
            } catch (e) {
              json = data;
            }
            resolve({
              status: res.statusCode,
              headers: res.headers,
              body: json
            });
          });
        });
    
        req.on('error', reject);
        if (body) {
          req.write(body);
        }
        req.end();
      });
    }

              { attempt_id: 'AT-101', criterion_id: 'RC-4', score: 2, feedback: 
'Clear consequence' }
            ],
            expected_revision: currentRev,
            operation_id: 'op_worksheet_ta_123'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(worksheetRes.status, 200);
        currentRev = worksheetRes.body.course_revision;
    
        // Check AT-101 is now graded
        const at101After = await request('GET', '/api/attempts/AT-101', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${taToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(at101After.body.attempt.status, 'graded');
        assert.strictEqual(at101After.body.attempt.rubric_score, 4);
    
        // Student Ben reading AT-101 must NOT see scores before release!
        const benAt101 = await request('GET', '/api/attempts/AT-101', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${benToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(benAt101.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(benAt101.body.attempt.feedback_status, 'hidden');
        assert.strictEqual(benAt101.body.attempt.objective_score, null);
        assert.strictEqual(benAt101.body.attempt.rubric_score, null);
        assert.strictEqual(benAt101.body.attempt.total_score, null);
    
        // 7. Batch Release Preview and Commit
        console.log('Test 7: Batch Release');
        const previewRes = await request('POST', '/api/release/preview', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${instructorToken}` },
          body: {
            attempt_ids: ['AT-101']
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(previewRes.status, 200);
        assert.ok(previewRes.body.preview_id);
        assert.strictEqual(previewRes.body.items.length, 1);
        const previewId = previewRes.body.preview_id;
    
        // Commit batch release
        const commitRes = await request('POST', '/api/release/commit', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${instructorToken}` },
          body: {
            preview_id: previewId,
            expected_revision: currentRev,
            operation_id: 'op_commit_release_123'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(commitRes.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(commitRes.body.released_count, 1);
        currentRev = commitRes.body.course_revision;
    
        // Ben reading AT-101 now sees released feedback!
        const benAt101Released = await request('GET', '/api/attempts/AT-101', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${benToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(benAt101Released.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(benAt101Released.body.attempt.feedback_status, 
'released');
        assert.strictEqual(benAt101Released.body.attempt.total_score, 4); // 0 
objective + 4 rubric
    
        // 8. Outcome Ledger & Policy & Exceptions
        console.log('Test 8: Outcome Ledger');
        const outcomesRes = await request('GET', '/api/outcomes', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${instructorToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(outcomesRes.status, 200);
        assert.ok(outcomesRes.body.ledger.length >= 2);
    
        // TA forbidden from outcome ledger
        const taOutcomes = await request('GET', '/api/outcomes', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${taToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(taOutcomes.status, 403);
    
        // Policy update: A-01: 30%, A-03: 40%, A-04: 20%, newAssessmentId: 10%
        const policyRes = await request('POST', '/api/policy', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${instructorToken}` },
          body: {
            weights: {
              'A-01': 30,
              'A-03': 40,
              'A-04': 20,
              [newAssessmentId]: 10
            },
            expected_revision: currentRev,
            operation_id: 'op_policy_update_123'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(policyRes.status, 200);
        currentRev = policyRes.body.course_revision;
    
        // Student exception (excuse Ben for A-01)
        const excuseRes = await request('POST', '/api/exceptions', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${instructorToken}` },
          body: {
            student_id: 'user_4',
            assessment_id: 'A-01',
            excused: true,
            reason: 'Medical accommodation approved',
            expected_revision: currentRev,
            operation_id: 'op_excuse_ben_123'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(excuseRes.status, 200);
        currentRev = excuseRes.body.course_revision;
    
        // 9. Audit log
        console.log('Test 9: Audit log');
        const auditRes = await request('GET', '/api/audit', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${instructorToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(auditRes.status, 200);
        assert.ok(auditRes.body.events.length > 0);
    
        console.log('All backend verification tests passed successfully!');
      } finally {
        if (server) {
          server.close();
        }
        if (fs.existsSync(testDbPath)) {
          fs.unlinkSync(testDbPath);
        }
      }
    }
    
    runTests().catch(err => {
      console.error('Test failed:', err);
      process.exit(1);
    });

+17|let server;
+18|let baseUrl;
+19|
+20|function request(method, path, options = {}) {
+21|  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+22|    const url = new URL(path, baseUrl);
+23|    const headers = options.headers || {};
+24|    let body = options.body;
+25|
+26|    if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
+27|      body = JSON.stringify(body);
+28|      headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
+29|    }
+30|
+31|    const req = http.request(url, {
+32|      method: method.toUpperCase(),
+33|      headers
+34|    }, (res) => {
+35|      let data = '';
+36|      res.on('data', chunk => { data += chunk; });
+37|      res.on('end', () => {
+38|        let json = null;
+39|        try {
+40|          json = JSON.parse(data);
+41|        } catch (e) {
+42|          json = data;
+43|        }
+44|        resolve({
+45|          status: res.statusCode,
+46|          headers: res.headers,
+47|          body: json
+48|        });
+49|      });
+50|    });
+51|
+52|    req.on('error', reject);
+53|    if (body) {
+54|      req.write(body);
+55|    }
+56|    req.end();
+57|  });
+58|}

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Fix Express 5 wildcard route in server.js

Reasoning:
**Express 5.1.0 and Path Wildcards: A Quick Fix**

Okay, so it looks like Express 5.1.0's updated `path-to-regexp` is throwing a 
curveball with how it handles wildcards. Apparently, a straight `*` in a route 
path is no longer considered valid unless I explicitly define it as `/(.*)` or 
as `*splat`, or I resort to a middleware solution. This could definitely break 
some of my existing routes, especially if I have any that rely on that simple 
wildcard for serving static files.

Thinking quickly, I'm going to patch `/app/server.js` with a clever middleware 
solution. It looks like the best approach is to intercept the requests. I'll 
insert a check at the beginning, like this: `app.use((req, res, next) => { if 
(req.path.startsWith('/api')) return next(); res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 
'public', 'index.html')); });`. This will ensure that any request path that 
begins with `/api` is passed to the next route handler, so I can keep my API 
routes functional. For all other requests, I'll serve `index.html` from the 
`public` directory, taking care of that wildcard functionality. It's a clean and
efficient way to handle this breaking change. Good, this should get me back on 
track!




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/server.js"
  old_str: 
    // SPA fallback for HTML5 history navigation if any
    app.get('*', (req, res, next) => {
      if (req.path.startsWith('/api')) {
        return next();
      }
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });

  new_str: 
    // SPA fallback for non-API routes
    app.use((req, res, next) => {
      if (req.path.startsWith('/api')) {
        return next();
      }
      if (req.method === 'GET') {
        return res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
      }
      next();
    });

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create accessible index.html markup

Reasoning:
**Okay, let's break down this UI/UX plan. I need to make sure this frontend is 
robust, accessible, and user-friendly, catering to different roles and needs 
within the course management system.**

First, I need to nail down the authentication flow. We'll be using local storage
for the token, hitting the `/api/auth/me` endpoint on page load to check for a 
valid session. I'll need to handle the sign-in form with email and password, 
offering quick-select demo accounts for easy testing. Error handling is crucial;
invalid logins and network issues need clear, visible alerts.

Next, the visual structure. A persistent top header needs to display the 
application brand and course details, along with user info (name, email, role), 
the current fixed reference time, course revision, sync status, a refresh 
button, and a sign-out button.

The core navigation is critical. A main navigation bar with clear, accessible 
tabs (Courses, Assessments, Attempts, Gradebook, Audit) will provide access to 
each major section. The active tab needs visual highlighting, ARIA labels, and 
keyboard support for a good user experience.

Now, let's delve into the individual views.

*   **Courses:** An overview card with essential course metadata and a roster 
table, showcasing enrolled users, including staff and students with 
accommodations. Summary stats are also important for a quick snapshot.
*   **Assessments:** I'll need a way to filter assessments by status (Draft, 
Published), a clear listing with vital information like title, dates, duration, 
attempts, and points. Differentiated actions based on user role are necessary, 
with buttons for creating, managing, and publishing assessments for staff and 
start/continue attempts and view results for students. Modals for creating and 
editing assessments will need careful design for accessibility and usability.
*   **Attempts:** Another list view with filtering (by Assessment, Status), 
differentiating display based on the user's role. It will need action buttons 
for staff to grade and students to continue. A modal/workspace for students 
while taking the attempt has to include the timer and item list, and also for 
grading needs to be clean, clear, with score inputs, feedback and a "Save grade"
option.
*   **Gradebook:** This is a complex area with multiple sub-views. The Outcome 
Ledger needs a weighting policy editor for staff, while also displaying an 
outcome ledger table for both instructors and students, showing assessment 
scores and final grade calculations. The Atomic Grading Worksheet (staff only) 
must permit a grader to select and grade multiple items simultaneously. Batch 
release with atomic save is key and must work via API.
*   **Audit Log:** This view will be the durable history of everything that's 
happened in the course.

Everything has to be keyboard-accessible and responsive, designed to be easily 
usable at different screen sizes. I'll need to pay close attention to labels, 
focus management, error notifications, and the synchronization process to ensure
the app handles stale data and conflicts gracefully. I'll need to design each 
section with appropriate accessibility features: ARIA attributes, focus rings, 
semantic HTML, and proper error and success announcements for screen reader 
users, with no horizontal scrolling at 375 pixels wide.

We'll be using local storage for the authentication token. It is crucial to 
manage synchronization and data consistency, specifically for the API endpoints 
for data writes and course revisioning.
