+433|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+434|  // View 2: Assessments
+435|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+436|  function renderAssessmentsView() {
+437|    const list = document.getElementById('assessments-list');
+438|    list.innerHTML = '';
+439|
+440|    if (state.assessments.length === 0) {
+441|      list.innerHTML = '<p class="text-muted">No assessments 
available.</p>';
+442|      return;
+443|    }
+444|
+445|    const isInstructor = state.user.role === 'instructor';
+446|    const isStudent = state.user.role === 'student';
+447|
+448|    for (const a of state.assessments) {
+449|      const card = document.createElement('div');
+450|      card.className = `card assessment-card status-${a.status}`;
+451|
+452|      const statusBadge = `<span class="badge 
badge-status-${a.status}">${escapeHtml(a.status.toUpperCase())}</span>`;
+453|      
+454|      let studentInfoHtml = '';
+455|      if (isStudent && a.student_info) {
+456|        const sInfo = a.student_info;
+457|        studentInfoHtml = `
+458|          <div class="mt-2 p-2" style="background:#f8fafc; 
border-radius:4px; font-size:0.8125rem;">
+459|            <div><strong>Effective Due:</strong> 
${escapeHtml(formatDate(sInfo.effective_due_at))}</div>
+460|            <div><strong>Effective Duration:</strong> 
${sInfo.effective_duration_minutes} min (${sInfo.extra_time_minutes > 0 ? '+' + 
sInfo.extra_time_minutes + 'm extra' : 'standard'})</div>
+461|            <div><strong>Attempts Used:</strong> ${sInfo.attempts_used} / 
${a.max_attempts}</div>
+462|          </div>
+463|        `;
+464|      }
+465|
+466|      let actionsHtml = '';
+467|      if (isStudent && a.student_info) {
+468|        const sInfo = a.student_info;
+469|        if (sInfo.has_active_attempt) {
+470|          const activeAtt = sInfo.attempts.find(att => att.status === 
'in_progress');
+471|          actionsHtml += `<button type="button" class="btn btn-primary 
btn-sm" onclick="window.openAttemptModal('${activeAtt?.id}')">Resume Active 
Attempt</button>`;
+472|        } else if (sInfo.can_start) {
+473|          actionsHtml += `<button type="button" class="btn btn-primary 
btn-sm" onclick="window.startNewAttempt('${a.id}')">Start Attempt</button>`;
+474|        } else if (sInfo.attempts.length > 0) {
+475|          const lastAtt = sInfo.attempts[sInfo.attempts.length - 1];
+476|          actionsHtml += `<button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary 
btn-sm" onclick="window.openAttemptModal('${lastAtt.id}')">View Attempt 
(${lastAtt.status})</button>`;
+477|        }
+478|      }
+479|
+480|      if (isInstructor && a.status === 'draft') {
+481|        actionsHtml += `
+482|          <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" 
onclick="window.openAddItemModal('${a.id}')">+ Add Question</button>
+483|          <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" 
onclick="window.publishAssessment('${a.id}')">Publish</button>
+484|        `;
+485|      }
+486|
+487|      card.innerHTML = `
+488|        <div>
+489|          <div class="assessment-card-header">
+490|            <div>
+491|              <h3>${escapeHtml(a.title)}</h3>
+492|              <small class="text-muted">ID: ${escapeHtml(a.id)} | Weight: 
${a.weight_percent}%</small>
+493|            </div>
+494|            ${statusBadge}
+495|          </div>
+496|          <div class="assessment-meta-grid">
+497|            <div><strong>Opens:</strong> 
${escapeHtml(formatDate(a.opens_at))}</div>
+498|            <div><strong>Due:</strong> 
${escapeHtml(formatDate(a.due_at))}</div>
+499|            <div><strong>Duration:</strong> ${a.duration_minutes} 
mins</div>
+500|            <div><strong>Questions:</strong> ${a.item_count} 
(${a.total_points} pts)</div>
+501|          </div>
+502|          ${studentInfoHtml}
+503|        </div>
+504|        <div class="assessment-actions">
+505|          ${actionsHtml}
+506|        </div>
+507|      `;
+508|      list.appendChild(card);
+509|    }
+510|  }
+511|
+512|  function formatDate(isoStr) {
+513|    if (!isoStr) return 'N/A';
+514|    try {
+515|      const d = new Date(isoStr);
+516|      return d.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').substring(0, 16) + ' UTC';
+517|    } catch (e) {
+518|      return isoStr;
+519|    }
+520|  }

+522|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+523|  // View 3: Attempts
+524|  // -------------------------------------------------------------
+525|  function renderAttemptsView() {
+526|    const tbody = document.getElementById('attempts-table-body');
+527|    tbody.innerHTML = '';
+528|
+529|    const filterStatus = 
document.getElementById('attempts-filter-status').value;
+530|    let filtered = state.attempts;
+531|    if (filterStatus !== 'all') {
+532|      filtered = filtered.filter(a => a.status === filterStatus);
+533|    }
+534|
+535|    if (filtered.length === 0) {
+536|      tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="9" class="text-muted 
text-center">No attempts match the current filter.</td></tr>';
+537|      return;
+538|    }
+539|
+540|    const isStaff = state.user.role === 'instructor' || state.user.role ===
'teaching_assistant';
+541|
+542|    for (const att of filtered) {
+543|      const tr = document.createElement('tr');
+544|
+545|      const isReleased = att.feedback_status === 'released';
+546|      let scoreDisplay = '<span class="text-muted">Pending release</span>';
+547|      if (isReleased) {
+548|        scoreDisplay = `<strong>${att.total_score} pts</strong>`;
+549|      } else if (isStaff) {
+550|        scoreDisplay = `<span class="text-muted">${att.total_score !== null
? att.total_score + ' pts (Private)' : 'Ungraded'}</span>`;
+551|      }
+552|
+553|      let actionsHtml = `<button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary 
btn-sm" onclick="window.openAttemptModal('${att.id}')">View</button>`;
+554|      if (isStaff && (att.status === 'submitted' || att.status === 
'graded') && !isReleased) {
+555|        actionsHtml += ` <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary 
btn-sm ml-2" onclick="window.openAttemptModal('${att.id}')">Grade</button>`;
+556|      }
+557|
+558|      tr.innerHTML = `
+559|        <td><strong>${escapeHtml(att.id)}</strong></td>
+560|        <td>${escapeHtml(att.student_name || att.student_id)}</td>
+561|        <td>${escapeHtml(att.assessment_title || att.assessment_id)}</td>
+562|        <td><span class="badge 
badge-info">${escapeHtml(att.status.toUpperCase())}</span></td>
+563|        <td>${escapeHtml(formatDate(att.started_at))}</td>
+564|        <td>${escapeHtml(formatDate(att.submitted_at))}</td>
+565|        <td><span class="badge ${isReleased ? 'badge-released' : 
'badge-pending'}">${escapeHtml(att.feedback_status.toUpperCase())}</span></td>
+566|        <td>${scoreDisplay}</td>
+567|        <td>${actionsHtml}</td>
+568|      `;
+569|      tbody.appendChild(tr);
+570|    }
+571|  }
+572|

document.getElementById('attempts-filter-status').addEventListener('change', () 
=> {
+574|    renderAttemptsView();
+575|  });

+604|    } else if (state.gradebookSubview === 'gb-worksheet') {

+611|  // Outcome Ledger
+612|  function renderOutcomeLedger() {
+613|    const container = document.getElementById('ledger-content-container');
+614|    if (!state.outcomes || !state.outcomes.ledger) {
+615|      container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-muted">Loading outcome 
data...</p>';
+616|      return;
+617|    }
+618|
+619|    const isInstructor = state.user.role === 'instructor';
+620|    const isStudent = state.user.role === 'student';
+621|
+622|    if (isStudent) {
+623|      // Student personal view
+624|      const myRow = state.outcomes.ledger[0];
+625|      if (!myRow) {
+626|        container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-muted">No grade records 
found.</p>';
+627|        return;
+628|      }
+629|
+630|      let finalScoreText = 'Pending / Unavailable';
+631|      if (myRow.final_status === 'calculated' && myRow.final_percentage !==
null) {
+632|        finalScoreText = `${myRow.final_percentage.toFixed(2)}%`;
+633|      }
+634|
+635|      let tableRows = '';
+636|      for (const cell of myRow.assessments) {
+637|        let scoreText = 'Pending';
+638|        if (cell.state === 'released') {
+639|          scoreText = `${cell.awarded_points} / ${cell.max_points} pts`;
+640|        } else if (cell.state === 'excused') {
+641|          scoreText = `Excused (${escapeHtml(cell.excuse_reason)})`;
+642|        } else if (cell.state === 'missing') {
+643|          scoreText = 'Missing';
+644|        } else if (cell.state === 'unweighted') {
+645|          scoreText = 'Unweighted (0%)';
+646|        }
+647|
+648|        tableRows += `
+649|          <tr>
+650|            <td><strong>${escapeHtml(cell.assessment_title)}</strong></td>
+651|            <td>${cell.weight.toFixed(2)}%</td>
+652|            <td><span class="badge 
badge-${cell.state}">${cell.state.toUpperCase()}</span></td>
+653|            <td>${scoreText}</td>
+654|          </tr>
+655|        `;
+656|      }
+657|
+658|      container.innerHTML = `
+659|        <div class="card mb-4">
+660|          <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center 
mb-3">
+661|            <div>
+662|              <h3>Final Weighted Outcome</h3>
+663|              <p class="text-muted">${escapeHtml(myRow.explanation)}</p>
+664|            </div>
+665|            <div style="font-size: 1.75rem; font-weight: 700; color: 
var(--color-primary);">
+666|              ${finalScoreText}
+667|            </div>
+668|          </div>
+669|          <div class="table-responsive">
+670|            <table class="data-table">
+671|              <thead>
+672|                <tr>
+673|                  <th scope="col">Assessment</th>
+674|                  <th scope="col">Policy Weight</th>
+675|                  <th scope="col">Status</th>
+676|                  <th scope="col">Awarded Score</th>
+677|                </tr>
+678|              </thead>
+679|              <tbody>
+680|                ${tableRows}
+681|              </tbody>
+682|            </table>
+683|          </div>
+684|        </div>
+685|      `;
+686|      return;
+687|    }
+688|
+689|    // Instructor full ledger
+690|    const searchVal = 
document.getElementById('student-search-input')?.value.toLowerCase().trim() || 
'';
+691|    const filteredLedger = state.outcomes.ledger.filter(row => {
+692|      if (!searchVal) return true;
+693|      return row.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchVal) || 
row.email.toLowerCase().includes(searchVal);
+694|    });
+695|
+696|    const assessmentsHeaders = state.outcomes.assessments.map(a => `
+697|      <th scope="col" style="min-width: 140px;">
+698|        <div>${escapeHtml(a.title)}</div>
+699|        <small class="text-muted">${a.weight.toFixed(2)}% | ${a.max_points}
pts</small>
+700|      </th>
+701|    `).join('');
+702|
+703|    let rowsHtml = '';
+704|    for (const row of filteredLedger) {
+705|      let finalDisplay = '<span class="text-muted">Pending</span>';
+706|      if (row.final_status === 'calculated' && row.final_percentage !== 
null) {
+707|        finalDisplay = `<strong style="color: 
var(--color-primary);">${row.final_percentage.toFixed(2)}%</strong>`;
+708|      }
+709|
+710|      const cellsHtml = row.assessments.map(cell => {
+711|        let cellContent = '';
+712|        if (cell.state === 'released') {
+713|          cellContent = 
`<strong>${cell.awarded_points}/${cell.max_points}</strong> <span class="badge 
badge-released">Released</span>`;
+714|        } else if (cell.state === 'excused') {
+715|          cellContent = `<span class="badge badge-excused" 
title="${escapeHtml(cell.excuse_reason)}">Excused</span>`;
+716|        } else if (cell.state === 'missing') {
+717|          cellContent = `<span class="badge badge-missing">Missing</span>`;
+718|        } else if (cell.state === 'unweighted') {
+719|          cellContent = `<span class="badge badge-unweighted">0% 
Wt</span>`;
+720|        } else {
+721|          cellContent = `<span class="badge badge-pending">Pending</span>`;
+722|        }
+723|
+724|        const excuseBtn = `
+725|          <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary mt-1" 
style="font-size:0.7rem; padding:2px 6px; min-height:24px;"
+726|            onclick="window.openExceptionModal('${row.student_id}', 
'${cell.assessment_id}', '${escapeHtml(row.name)}', 
'${escapeHtml(cell.assessment_title)}', ${cell.excused})">
+727|            ${cell.excused ? 'Restore' : 'Excuse'}
+728|          </button>
+729|        `;
+730|
+731|        return `
+732|          <td>
+733|            <div class="outcome-cell">
+734|              ${cellContent}
+735|              ${excuseBtn}
+736|            </div>
+737|          </td>
+738|        `;
+739|      }).join('');
+740|
+741|      rowsHtml += `
+742|        <tr>
+743|          <td>
+744|            <strong>${escapeHtml(row.name)}</strong>
+745|            <div class="text-muted" 
style="font-size:0.75rem;">${escapeHtml(row.email)}</div>
+746|          </td>
+747|          ${cellsHtml}
+748|          <td>${finalDisplay}</td>
+749|        </tr>
+750|      `;
+751|    }
+752|
+753|    container.innerHTML = `
+754|      <div class="table-responsive">
+755|        <table class="data-table">
+756|          <thead>
+757|            <tr>
+758|              <th scope="col" style="min-width: 160px;">Student</th>
+759|              ${assessmentsHeaders}
+760|              <th scope="col" style="min-width: 110px;">Final Grade</th>
+761|            </tr>
+762|          </thead>
+763|          <tbody>
+764|            ${rowsHtml}
+765|          </tbody>
+766|        </table>
+767|      </div>
+768|    `;
+769|  }

document.getElementById('student-search-input')?.addEventListener('input', () =>
{
+772|    renderOutcomeLedger();
+773|  });
+774|
+775|  // Atomic Worksheet
+776|  async function renderWorksheet() {
+777|    const tbody = document.getElementById('worksheet-table-body');
+778|    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-muted">Loading 
worksheet data...</td></tr>';
+779|
+780|    const isStaff = state.user.role === 'instructor' || state.user.role ===
'teaching_assistant';
+781|    if (!isStaff) return;
+782|
+783|    // We fetch detailed attempts that are submitted or graded but 
unreleased
+784|    const unreleased = state.attempts.filter(a => (a.status === 'submitted'
|| a.status === 'graded') && a.feedback_status !== 'released');
+785|    
+786|    if (unreleased.length === 0) {
+787|      tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-muted">No 
unreleased attempts awaiting worksheet grading.</td></tr>';
+788|      return;
+789|    }
+790|
+791|    // Load full details for these attempts
+792|    const rowsData = [];
+793|    for (const att of unreleased) {
+794|      if (state.user.role === 'teaching_assistant' && 
att.assigned_grader_id !== state.user.id) {
+795|        continue;
+796|      }
+797|      try {
+798|        const detail = await apiCall('GET', `/api/attempts/${att.id}`);
+799|        const attempt = detail.attempt;
+800|        for (const item of attempt.items) {
+801|          if (item.kind === 'written' && item.rubric_criteria) {
+802|            for (const rc of item.rubric_criteria) {
+803|              rowsData.push({
+804|                attempt_id: attempt.id,
+805|                student_name: attempt.student_name,
+806|                assessment_title: attempt.assessment_title,
+807|                criterion_id: rc.id,
+808|                criterion_label: rc.label,
+809|                max_points: rc.max_points,
+810|                current_score: rc.grade ? rc.grade.score : '',
+811|                current_feedback: rc.grade ? rc.grade.feedback : ''
+812|              });
+813|            }
+814|          }
+815|        }
+816|      } catch (e) {
+817|        console.warn(`Error loading attempt ${att.id}:`, e);
+818|      }
+819|    }
+820|
+821|    if (rowsData.length === 0) {
+822|      tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-muted">No rubric 
criteria available for worksheet grading.</td></tr>';
+823|      return;
+824|    }
+825|
+826|    tbody.innerHTML = '';
+827|    for (let i = 0; i < rowsData.length; i++) {
+828|      const r = rowsData[i];
+829|      const tr = document.createElement('tr');
+830|      tr.innerHTML = `
+831|        <td>
+832|          <input type="checkbox" class="ws-row-check" data-idx="${i}" 
aria-label="Select row ${r.attempt_id} ${r.criterion_id}">
+833|        </td>
+834|        <td><strong>${escapeHtml(r.attempt_id)}</strong></td>
+835|        <td>${escapeHtml(r.student_name)}</td>
+836|        <td>${escapeHtml(r.assessment_title)}</td>
+837|        <td>${escapeHtml(r.criterion_label)} <span class="badge 
badge-neutral">Max: ${r.max_points}</span></td>
+838|        <td>
+839|          <input type="number" class="input-control ws-score" 
data-idx="${i}" min="0" max="${r.max_points}" step="any" 
value="${r.current_score}" placeholder="0 - ${r.max_points}">
+840|        </td>
+841|        <td>
+842|          <input type="text" class="input-control ws-feedback" 
data-idx="${i}" value="${escapeHtml(r.current_feedback || '')}" 
placeholder="Optional feedback...">
+843|        </td>
+844|      `;
+845|      tbody.appendChild(tr);
+846|    }
+847|
+848|    // Attach row change listeners
+849|    window.currentWorksheetRowsData = rowsData;
+850|    updateWorksheetSelectionCount();
+851|
+852|    tbody.querySelectorAll('.ws-row-check').forEach(cb => {
+853|      cb.addEventListener('change', updateWorksheetSelectionCount);
+854|    });
+855|  }
+856|
+857|  function updateWorksheetSelectionCount() {
+858|    const selected = document.querySelectorAll('.ws-row-check:checked');
+859|    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-worksheet');
+860|    const note = document.getElementById('worksheet-status-note');
+861|
+862|    btn.disabled = selected.length === 0;
+863|    note.textContent = `${selected.length} row(s) selected`;
+864|  }
+865|
+866|  document.getElementById('worksheet-form')?.addEventListener('submit', 
async (e) => {
+867|    e.preventDefault();
+868|    const checked = 
Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.ws-row-check:checked'));
+869|    if (checked.length === 0) return;
+870|
+871|    const rowsPayload = [];
+872|    for (const cb of checked) {
+873|      const idx = Number(cb.getAttribute('data-idx'));
+874|      const rData = window.currentWorksheetRowsData[idx];
+875|      const scoreInput = 
document.querySelector(`.ws-score[data-idx="${idx}"]`);
+876|      const feedbackInput = 
document.querySelector(`.ws-feedback[data-idx="${idx}"]`);
+877|
+878|      const scoreVal = scoreInput.value.trim();
+879|      if (scoreVal === '') {
+880|        showToast(`Please enter a score for attempt ${rData.attempt_id} 
criterion ${rData.criterion_id}`, 'error');
+881|        scoreInput.focus();
+882|        return;
+883|      }
+884|
+885|      rowsPayload.push({
+886|        attempt_id: rData.attempt_id,
+887|        criterion_id: rData.criterion_id,
+888|        score: Number(scoreVal),
+889|        feedback: feedbackInput.value.trim()
+890|      });
+891|    }
+892|
+893|    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-worksheet');
+894|    btn.disabled = true;
+895|    btn.textContent = 'Saving Worksheet...';
+896|
+897|    try {
+898|      await apiCall('POST', '/api/grading/worksheet', { rows: rowsPayload 
}, true);
+899|      showToast('Worksheet grades saved successfully!', 'success');
+900|      await refreshWorkspaceData(true);
+901|      renderWorksheet();
+902|    } catch (err) {
+903|      showToast(err.data?.error || err.message || 'Worksheet save failed', 
'error');
+904|    } finally {
+905|      btn.disabled = false;
+906|      btn.textContent = 'Save Selected Worksheet Rows';
+907|    }
+908|  });
+909|
+910|  // Batch Release
+911|  function renderBatchRelease() {
+912|    const tbody = document.getElementById('release-table-body');
+913|    tbody.innerHTML = '';
+914|
+915|    const gradedUnreleased = state.attempts.filter(a => a.status === 
'graded' && a.feedback_status !== 'released');
+916|    if (gradedUnreleased.length === 0) {
+917|      tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="text-muted">No fully 
graded unreleased attempts available for release.</td></tr>';
+918|      document.getElementById('btn-preview-batch-release').disabled = true;
+919|      return;
+920|    }
+921|
+922|    for (const att of gradedUnreleased) {
+923|      const tr = document.createElement('tr');
+924|      tr.innerHTML = `
+925|        <td>
+926|          <input type="checkbox" class="release-item-check" 
value="${att.id}" aria-label="Select attempt ${att.id}">
+927|        </td>
+928|        <td><strong>${escapeHtml(att.id)}</strong></td>
+929|        <td>${escapeHtml(att.student_name)} 
(${escapeHtml(att.student_email)})</td>
+930|        <td>${escapeHtml(att.assessment_title)}</td>
+931|        <td>${att.objective_score} pts</td>
+932|        <td>${att.rubric_score} pts</td>
+933|        <td><strong>${att.total_score} pts</strong></td>
+934|        <td>
+935|          <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" 
onclick="window.releaseSingleAttempt('${att.id}')">Release Single</button>
+936|        </td>
+937|      `;
+938|      tbody.appendChild(tr);
+939|    }
+940|
+941|    const selectAll = document.getElementById('release-select-all');
+942|    selectAll.checked = false;
+943|    selectAll.onchange = () => {
+944|      document.querySelectorAll('.release-item-check').forEach(cb => {
+945|        cb.checked = selectAll.checked;
+946|      });
+947|      updateBatchReleaseCount();
+948|    };
+949|
+950|    tbody.querySelectorAll('.release-item-check').forEach(cb => {
+951|      cb.onchange = updateBatchReleaseCount;
+952|    });
+953|
+954|    updateBatchReleaseCount();
+955|  }
+956|
+957|  function updateBatchReleaseCount() {
+958|    const checked = 
document.querySelectorAll('.release-item-check:checked');
+959|    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-preview-batch-release');
+960|    btn.disabled = checked.length === 0;
+961|    btn.textContent = `Preview Selected for Release (${checked.length} 
selected)`;
+962|  }
+963|
+964|  
document.getElementById('btn-preview-batch-release')?.addEventListener('click', 
async () => {
+965|    const checked = 
Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.release-item-check:checked')).map(cb => 
cb.value);
+966|    if (checked.length === 0) return;
+967|
+968|    try {
+969|      const data = await apiCall('POST', '/api/release/preview', { 
attempt_ids: checked });
+970|      state.batchReleasePreviewId = data.preview_id;
+971|
+972|      const previewTbody = 
document.getElementById('release-preview-table-body');
+973|      previewTbody.innerHTML = '';
+974|
+975|      for (const item of data.items) {
+976|        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
+977|        tr.innerHTML = `
+978|          <td><strong>${escapeHtml(item.attempt_id)}</strong></td>
+979|          <td>${escapeHtml(item.student_name)}</td>
+980|          <td>${escapeHtml(item.student_email)}</td>
+981|          <td>${escapeHtml(item.assessment_title)}</td>
+982|          <td>${item.objective_score} pts</td>
+983|          <td>${item.rubric_score} pts</td>
+984|          <td><strong>${item.total_score} / ${item.max_score} 
pts</strong></td>
+985|        `;
+986|        previewTbody.appendChild(tr);
+987|      }
+988|
+989|      openModal('modal-release-preview', 
document.getElementById('btn-preview-batch-release'));
+990|    } catch (err) {
+991|      showToast(err.data?.error || err.message || 'Failed to generate 
preview', 'error');
+992|    }
+993|  });
+994|
+995|  
document.getElementById('btn-confirm-batch-release')?.addEventListener('click', 
async () => {
+996|    if (!state.batchReleasePreviewId) return;
+997|
+998|    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-confirm-batch-release');
+999|    btn.disabled = true;
+1000|    btn.textContent = 'Committing Release...';

attempt(s)!`, 'success');

+1008|    } catch (err) {

commit failed';
+1011|      errEl.classList.remove('hidden');
+1012|    } finally {
+1013|      btn.disabled = false;
+1014|      btn.textContent = 'Confirm & Commit Release';
+1015|    }

+1018|  window.releaseSingleAttempt = async function(attemptId) {
+1019|    if (!confirm(`Release grades and feedback for attempt ${attemptId}?`))
return;
+1020|
+1021|    try {
+1022|      await apiCall('POST', '/api/release/single', { attempt_id: attemptId
}, true);
+1023|      showToast(`Attempt ${attemptId} released successfully!`, 'success');
+1024|      await refreshWorkspaceData(true);
+1025|      renderBatchRelease();
+1026|    } catch (err) {
+1027|      showToast(err.data?.error || err.message || 'Release failed', 
'error');
+1028|    }
+1029|  };

+1569|  window.saveSingleRubricGrade = async function(attemptId, criterionId) {

    # Coursemark Application Manifest
    
    ## Start Command
    
    ```bash
    node /app/server.js
    ```
    
    SQLite path: /app/coursemark.db
    
    ## Endpoints and Routes
    
    ### Health Check
    - `GET /api/health` - Public health check and service status
    
    ### Authentication & Sessions
    - `POST /api/auth/login` - User sign-in, issues SQLite-persisted bearer 
token
    - `POST /api/auth/logout` - User sign-out, revokes all active session tokens
for the account
    - `GET /api/auth/me` - Read authenticated user profile and course revision 
state
    
    ### Courses & Overview
    - `GET /api/courses` - Read enrolled course BIO-214 overview, roster, and 
accommodations
    
    ### Assessment Authoring & Availability
    - `GET /api/assessments` - Read course assessments (role-filtered: staff 
sees drafts & published; students see published only)
    - `GET /api/assessments/:id` - Read assessment details and questions (staff 
sees answer keys; students never receive answer keys)
    - `POST /api/assessments` - Create draft assessment (instructor only)
    - `POST /api/assessments/:id/items` - Add multiple-choice or written 
question with rubric criteria to draft (instructor only)
    - `POST /api/assessments/:id/publish` - Publish draft assessment with 
terminal status and 0% initial weight (instructor only)
    
    ### Timed Attempts & Answers
    - `GET /api/attempts` - Read attempts (role-isolated: instructor sees all, 
TA sees assigned, student sees own with unreleased scores hidden)
    - `GET /api/attempts/:id` - Read attempt details, items, student answers, 
and grading/feedback
    - `POST /api/attempts/start` - Start timed assessment attempt applying 
student accommodations (student only)
    - `POST /api/attempts/:id/answers` - Save in-progress answers before expiry 
(student only)
    - `POST /api/attempts/:id/submit` - Terminal submission of attempt with 
auto-objective scoring (student only)
    
    ### Grading & Rubrics
    - `POST /api/grading/rubric` - Save single rubric criterion score and 
feedback (instructor or assigned TA)
    - `POST /api/grading/worksheet` - Atomic multi-row rubric worksheet grading 
with single revision increment (instructor or assigned TA)
    
    ### Feedback Release
    - `POST /api/release/single` - Release scores and feedback for single graded
attempt (instructor only)
    - `POST /api/release/preview` - Preview recipient details, assessment 
titles, and awarded totals for reviewed batch release (instructor only)
    - `POST /api/release/commit` - Atomic reviewed batch release commit bound to
revision (instructor only)
    
    ### Outcome Ledger, Policy & Exceptions
    - `GET /api/outcomes` - Read weighted outcome ledger (instructor sees all 
students; students see own outcome row; TA forbidden)
    - `POST /api/policy` - Update assessment outcome percentage weighting policy
(instructor only)
    - `POST /api/exceptions` - Excuse or restore student assessment with 
required reason (instructor only)
    
    ### Durable Audit Logging
    - `GET /api/audit` - Read durable newest-first audit events (role-filtered 
without score leakage)
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

**End-to-End Test Suite Deep Dive**

Okay, so I'm thinking, let's craft a really robust end-to-end test suite at 
`/app/test/e2e.test.js` to ensure everything functions perfectly. I want this to
be absolutely thorough, so I'm going to cover all the key aspects of this 
system, all the critical functionality and all the possible failure cases.

First, a quick health check: I'll verify that `GET /api/health` works as 
expected, returns a 200 with an "ok" status and a reference moment, and 
crucially, doesn't need a token to work.

Then, authentication and authorization: I'll simulate sign-in for different 
users (Ada, Luis, Nora, and Ben) with diverse roles and make sure their bearer 
tokens are correctly stored in SQLite. The password ("Coursemark!2026") needs to
be verified. Role-based access control is paramount. I'll meticulously test role
isolation for all four accounts.

Next, sign-out and token revocation: I'll ensure tokens are revoked across 
sessions and that a revoked token results in a 401 Unauthorized error.

Concurrency and optimistic locking is a crucial area. I'm going to test the 
revision system: make sure revisions start at 0, increment correctly with each 
successful write, and that stale writes (incorrect `expected_revision`) are 
rejected with a 409 Conflict error, leaving the data unchanged.

Idempotency and operation identifiers are important too. I need to make sure 
that a retry of the exact same operation with the same operation ID returns the 
same response without bumping the revision or creating another audit event. I'll
test that a mismatch in input, path, or method with the same operation ID 
results in a 409 Conflict. Another user reusing the same operation ID? That's 
also a 409.

Assessment authoring and validation: This is a significant piece. I need to 
ensure non-instructors can't create assessments, items, or publish assessments 
and that they get a 403 Forbidden error. I need to enforce that the "due before 
opens" rule triggers a 400 Bad Request error. I'll check multiple-choice 
question validation (no duplicate options, correct answer within options) and 
written question validation (rubric criteria should be non-empty). Also, the 
system must enforce that an assessment needs at least one item and a positive 
duration to be published and ensure assessments cannot be published or edited 
after publishing. And I'll verify the initial weight in the policy starts at 0%.

Timed attempts and accommodations: I'll test extra time and deadline extensions 
for specific students (Nora and Ben). I'll verify that the system correctly 
calculates adjusted durations and due dates. I'll confirm that the expiry 
instant correctly uses the earlier of the adjusted duration or adjusted due 
date, and that at or after expiry attempts are automatically submitted, answer 
writes are rejected, students can save in-progress answers and that they can 
only read their own. For submission, I need to confirm the system calculates MC 
scores, preserves written answers. Importantly, I will verify that before 
release, students see no scores, rubrics or feedback in attempts or audit log 
and that answer keys are never leaked to the student.

Rubric grading and atomic worksheets: Instructors can grade any attempt, but 
TA's can only grade assigned attempts. TA's grading unassigned attempts or 
students grading any attempt should trigger a 403 Forbidden error. I'll check 
single-row rubric grade saves, and that atomic worksheet grading across multiple
rows/attempts works correctly: one revision bump, one audit event per attempt. 
I'll ensure invalid scores (negative, > max, non-numeric) are rejected with a 
400 Bad Request error. And finally, an attempt should become 'graded' once all 
rubric criteria are graded.

Reviewed batch release: Non-instructors shouldn't be able to release. Previewing
a batch release will need to correctly return a preview handle bound to the 
instructor and revision, without actually releasing anything or bumping the 
revision. I need to test that intervening course writes invalidate the preview 
and that committing a stale preview results in a 409 Conflict. A valid commit 
must release selected attempts atomically, bump the revision once, and write one
audit event per attempt. Released attempts should then be immutable. And, 
students need to see the released scores and feedback.

Weighted outcome ledger: The policy update needs to ensure the weights exactly 
sum to 100% and include all published assessments. I'll thoroughly test the 
final grade formula using the `outcomes.md` logic: ensuring that missing or 
unreleased positive-weight work makes the final grade pending (null) and that 
excusing missing work correctly removes its weight, allowing remaining released 
work to calculate the final percentage. The formula is `1

    const assert = require('assert');
    const http = require('http');
    const path = require('path');
    const fs = require('fs');
    
    // Use a dedicated clean test database
    const testDbPath = path.join(__dirname, 'test_e2e_coursemark.db');
    if (fs.existsSync(testDbPath)) {
      fs.unlinkSync(testDbPath);
    }
    process.env.DB_PATH = testDbPath;
    
    const { initializeDatabase } = require('../src/db');
    const { app } = require('../server');
    
    let server;
    let baseUrl;
    
    function request(method, path, options = {}) {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const url = new URL(path, baseUrl);
        const headers = options.headers || {};
        let body = options.body;
    
        if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
          body = JSON.stringify(body);
          headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
        }
    
        const req = http.request(url, {
          method: method.toUpperCase(),
          headers
        }, (res) => {
          let data = '';
          res.on('data', chunk => { data += chunk; });
          res.on('end', () => {
            let json = null;
            try {
              json = JSON.parse(data);
            } catch (e) {
              json = data;
            }
            resolve({
              status: res.statusCode,
              headers: res.headers,
              body: json
            });
          });
        });
    
        req.on('error', reject);
        if (body) {
          req.write(body);
        }
        req.end();
      });
    }
    
    async function runE2ETests() {
      console.log('--- Starting Comprehensive Coursemark E2E Test Suite ---');
    
      await new Promise((resolve) => {

          baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
          console.log(`E2E Test Server listening at ${baseUrl}`);
          resolve();
        });
      });

            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_start_nora_new_ass'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(startNora.status, 201);
        const noraAttemptId = startNora.body.attempt.id;
        curRev = startNora.body.course_revision;
    
        // Student Nora saves answers
        // Find item IDs for newAssId
        const noraAssDetail = await request('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${newAssId}`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${activeNoraToken}` }
        });
        const itemsList = noraAssDetail.body.assessment.items;
        const mcItem = itemsList.find(i => i.kind === 'multiple_choice');
        const writtenItem = itemsList.find(i => i.kind === 'written');
    
        const saveAns = await request('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${noraAttemptId}/answers`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${activeNoraToken}` },
          body: {
            answers: [
              { item_id: mcItem.id, value: 'Shannon-Wiener' }, // correct (+4 
pts)
              { item_id: writtenItem.id, value: 'Fragmentation increases edge 
effects and limits gene flow.' }
            ],
            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_save_nora_answers_1'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(saveAns.status, 200);
        curRev = saveAns.body.course_revision;
    
        // Submit attempt
        const submitNora = await request('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${noraAttemptId}/submit`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${activeNoraToken}` },
          body: {
            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_submit_nora_attempt_1'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(submitNora.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(submitNora.body.attempt.status, 'submitted');
        assert.strictEqual(submitNora.body.attempt.feedback_status, 'hidden');
        curRev = submitNora.body.course_revision;
    
        // Student privacy check: Nora reading attempt must NOT see 
objective_score or total_score
        const readNoraAttempt = await request('GET', 
`/api/attempts/${noraAttemptId}`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${activeNoraToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(readNoraAttempt.body.attempt.feedback_status, 
'hidden');
        assert.strictEqual(readNoraAttempt.body.attempt.objective_score, null);
        assert.strictEqual(readNoraAttempt.body.attempt.rubric_score, null);
        assert.strictEqual(readNoraAttempt.body.attempt.total_score, null);
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // 6. Rubric Grading & Atomic Worksheets
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        console.log('[E2E 6] Rubric Grading & Atomic Worksheets');
        // Staff Ada views noraAttemptId
        const adaViewNora = await request('GET', 
`/api/attempts/${noraAttemptId}`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(adaViewNora.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(adaViewNora.body.attempt.objective_score, 4);
    
        const rubricCrit = adaViewNora.body.attempt.items.find(i => i.kind === 
'written').rubric_criteria;
        assert.strictEqual(rubricCrit.length, 2);
    
        // TA Luis grades assigned attempt AT-101 using worksheet
        const worksheetLuis = await request('POST', '/api/grading/worksheet', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${luisToken}` },
          body: {
            rows: [
              { attempt_id: 'AT-101', criterion_id: 'RC-3', score: 2, feedback: 
'Accurate model limitation' },
              { attempt_id: 'AT-101', criterion_id: 'RC-4', score: 3, feedback: 
'Thorough explanation' }
            ],
            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_ws_luis_at101'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(worksheetLuis.status, 200);
        curRev = worksheetLuis.body.course_revision;
    
        // Instructor Ada grades noraAttemptId using worksheet
        const worksheetAda = await request('POST', '/api/grading/worksheet', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` },
          body: {
            rows: [
              { attempt_id: noraAttemptId, criterion_id: rubricCrit[0].id, 
score: 3, feedback: 'Great point on edges' },
              { attempt_id: noraAttemptId, criterion_id: rubricCrit[1].id, 
score: 2.5, feedback: 'Clear gene flow analysis' }
            ],
            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_ws_ada_nora'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(worksheetAda.status, 200);
        curRev = worksheetAda.body.course_revision;
    
        // Check noraAttempt is now 'graded'
        const noraGraded = await request('GET', 
`/api/attempts/${noraAttemptId}`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(noraGraded.body.attempt.status, 'graded');
        assert.strictEqual(noraGraded.body.attempt.objective_score, 4);
        assert.strictEqual(noraGraded.body.attempt.rubric_score, 5.5);
        assert.strictEqual(noraGraded.body.attempt.total_score, 9.5);
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // 7. Reviewed Batch Release
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        console.log('[E2E 7] Reviewed Batch Release');
        // Preview release for AT-101 and noraAttemptId
        const releasePreview = await request('POST', '/api/release/preview', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` },
          body: {
            attempt_ids: ['AT-101', noraAttemptId]
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(releasePreview.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(releasePreview.body.items.length, 2);
        const prevHandle = releasePreview.body.preview_id;
    
        // Commit batch release
        const releaseCommit = await request('POST', '/api/release/commit', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` },
          body: {
            preview_id: prevHandle,
            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_commit_batch_release_both'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(releaseCommit.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(releaseCommit.body.released_count, 2);
        curRev = releaseCommit.body.course_revision;
    
        // Nora reading her attempt now sees released scores!
        const noraAfterRelease = await request('GET', 
`/api/attempts/${noraAttemptId}`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${activeNoraToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(noraAfterRelease.body.attempt.feedback_status, 
'released');
        assert.strictEqual(noraAfterRelease.body.attempt.total_score, 9.5);
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // 8. Outcome Ledger & Exceptions
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        console.log('[E2E 8] Outcome Ledger, Policy & Student Exceptions');
        // Policy update: A-01: 30%, A-03: 30%, A-04: 20%, newAssId: 20% -> 
total 100%
        const updatePolicy = await request('POST', '/api/policy', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` },
          body: {
            weights: {
              'A-01': 30,
              'A-03': 30,
              'A-04': 20,
              [newAssId]: 20
            },
            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_update_policy_e2e'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(updatePolicy.status, 200);
        curRev = updatePolicy.body.course_revision;
    
        // Student Nora checks her outcome ledger row
        const noraOutcomes = await request('GET', '/api/outcomes', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${activeNoraToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(noraOutcomes.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(noraOutcomes.body.ledger.length, 1);
        assert.strictEqual(noraOutcomes.body.ledger[0].student_id, 'user_3');
    
        // Excuse Nora for A-01 and A-04 to see calculated final grade
        const excuseNoraA01 = await request('POST', '/api/exceptions', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` },
          body: {
            student_id: 'user_3',
            assessment_id: 'A-01',
            excused: true,
            reason: 'Approved field conflict',
            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_excuse_nora_a01'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(excuseNoraA01.status, 200);
        curRev = excuseNoraA01.body.course_revision;
    
        const excuseNoraA04 = await request('POST', '/api/exceptions', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` },
          body: {
            student_id: 'user_3',
            assessment_id: 'A-04',
            excused: true,
            reason: 'Waived prerequisite check',
            expected_revision: curRev,
            operation_id: 'op_excuse_nora_a04'
          }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(excuseNoraA04.status, 200);
        curRev = excuseNoraA04.body.course_revision;
    
        // Now Nora has released scores for A-03 (AT-102: 5/10 = 50%, weight 
30%)
        // and newAssId (noraAttemptId: 9.5/10 = 95%, weight 20%).
        // Both included weights: 30 + 20 = 50.
        // Sum weighted = 30 * (5/10) + 20 * (9.5/10) = 15 + 19 = 34.
        // Final percentage = 100 * 34 / 50 = 68.00%.
        const noraCalculated = await request('GET', '/api/outcomes', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${activeNoraToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(noraCalculated.body.ledger[0].final_status, 
'calculated');
        assert.strictEqual(noraCalculated.body.ledger[0].final_percentage, 
68.00);
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // 9. Audit Logging & Privacy
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        console.log('[E2E 9] Durable Audit Log & Privacy');
        const adaAudit = await request('GET', '/api/audit', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adaToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(adaAudit.status, 200);
        assert.ok(adaAudit.body.events.length >= 10);
        // Newest first
        for (let i = 0; i < adaAudit.body.events.length - 1; i++) {
          assert.ok(adaAudit.body.events[i].id > adaAudit.body.events[i + 
1].id);
        }
    
        const noraAudit = await request('GET', '/api/audit', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${activeNoraToken}` }
        });
        assert.strictEqual(noraAudit.status, 200);
        // Student Nora only sees events relevant to user_3
        for (const ev of noraAudit.body.events) {
          assert.ok(ev.student_id === 'user_3' || ev.details.includes('user_3') 
|| ev.details.includes('Nora'));
        }
    
        console.log('--- ALL E2E CONTRACT AND REQUIREMENT TESTS PASSED 
PERFECTLY! ---');
      } finally {
        if (server) {
          server.close();
        }
        if (fs.existsSync(testDbPath)) {
          fs.unlinkSync(testDbPath);
        }
      }
    }
    
    runE2ETests().catch(err => {
      console.error('E2E Test Failure:', err);
      process.exit(1);
    });

+16|let server;
+17|let baseUrl;
+18|
+19|function request(method, path, options = {}) {
+20|  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+21|    const url = new URL(path, baseUrl);
+22|    const headers = options.headers || {};
+23|    let body = options.body;
+24|
+25|    if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
+26|      body = JSON.stringify(body);
+27|      headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
+28|    }
+29|
+30|    const req = http.request(url, {
+31|      method: method.toUpperCase(),
+32|      headers
+33|    }, (res) => {
+34|      let data = '';
+35|      res.on('data', chunk => { data += chunk; });
+36|      res.on('end', () => {
+37|        let json = null;
+38|        try {
+39|          json = JSON.parse(data);
+40|        } catch (e) {
+41|          json = data;
+42|        }
+43|        resolve({
+44|          status: res.statusCode,
+45|          headers: res.headers,
+46|          body: json
+47|        });
+48|      });
+49|    });
+50|
+51|    req.on('error', reject);
+52|    if (body) {
+53|      req.write(body);
+54|    }
+55|    req.end();
+56|  });
+57|}

  let events;
  if (req.user.role === 'instructor') {
    ...
  } else if (req.user.role === 'teaching_assistant') {
    ...
  } else {
    events = db.prepare(`
      SELECT a.id, a.timestamp, a.course_id, a.action, a.target_type, 
a.target_id, a.details, a.attempt_id, a.assessment_id
      FROM audit_events a
      WHERE a.course_id = 'BIO-214' AND a.student_id = ?

select list for student audit queries.

Summary: Test server on port 3000 and static root

Reasoning:
Okay, here's my summary, presented as a stream of thought:

**Streamlining My Command Execution**

Alright, I'm looking at this and thinking, "There's gotta be a more elegant 
way." It's inefficient to execute commands piecemeal, especially when there's a 
logical flow. I need to achieve the desired outcome with maximum efficiency. So,
my mind immediately jumps to the possibilities of command chaining. The key here
is to leverage the power of the shell. I should be able to combine this into a 
single, cohesive command. Now, I'm weighing my options: should I use the 
sequential execution operator, the semicolon (`;`)? That's the simplest, it just
executes each command regardless of the success or failure of the preceding 
ones. Or, do I want to impose a dependency based on success? The double 
ampersand (`&&`) might be the better choice. It ensures that the subsequent 
command only runs if the prior one exits with a zero status, indicating success.
Ultimately, the best choice depends on the specific logic I'm trying to 
implement.




$ node /app/server.js > /tmp/server.log 2>&1 & SERVER_PID=$!; sleep 1; curl -s 
http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health; echo ""; curl -s -I http://127.0.0.1:3000/ | 
head -n 5; kill $SERVER_PID; wait $SERVER_PID 2>/dev/null

verify that all files are actually readable by the intended actors. Gotta be 
sure the basics are covered before we get too fancy. Now, thinking about how 
this runner is supposed to operate, specifically that "unprivileged user" detail
in `overview.md`, it’s clear we're aiming for a secure setup. So, I need to set 
the appropriate `chmod` flags. `644` for files that need read access for 
everyone and write for the owner, and `755` for executables so the unprivileged 
user can *execute* a writable copy as specified. This balances security with the
app's functionality; preventing privilege escalation and data corruption by the 
unprivileged user. It's about finding that sweet spot where we have flexibility 
without sacrificing safety. Gotta nail this down before proceeding.

Finish with message:
We have built and verified the complete **Coursemark** assessment workspace for 
BIO-214: Ecology and Field Methods according to all specifications and contracts
in `/assets/instructions` and seed records in 
`/assets/artifacts/coursemark_seed.json`.

### Summary of Completed Implementation:

1. **Architecture & Persistence (`/app/server.js`, `/app/src/db.js`, 
`/app/coursemark.db`)**:
   - Built with Node.js, Express, and `better-sqlite3`.
   - Starts with `node /app/server.js` on port `3000` (listening on 
`0.0.0.0:3000`), serves `/app/public/index.html`, and persists to 
`/app/coursemark.db` (honoring `process.env.DB_PATH`).
   - Public health check available at `GET /api/health` without authentication.
   - Seed importer imports all seed users, course BIO-214, enrollments, 
accommodations, assessments, items, rubrics, attempts, answers, rubric grades, 
and initial weights (A-01: 40%, A-03: 40%, A-04: 20%) on first database 
creation. Subsequent restarts retain all data without re-seeding.

2. **Authentication & Multi-Session Revocation (`/app/src/auth.js`)**:
   - Real bearer token authentication stored in SQLite (`tokens` table).
   - Password for all seed accounts: `Coursemark!2026`.
   - Before sign-in, all course and student records remain completely hidden.
   - `POST /api/auth/logout` revokes every active session token for the account,
causing other open tabs to return to sign-in on their next protected action.
   - Role-based access control and strict isolation between Instructor 
(`user_1`), Teaching Assistant (`user_2`), and Students (`user_3`, `user_4`).

3. **Concurrency & Operation Idempotency (`/app/src/idempotency.js`, 
`/app/src/utils.js`)**:
   - Monotonically increasing safe integer course revision tracking.
   - Every accepted course write increments course revision by 1.
   - Optimistic concurrency control: requests send `expected_revision`. Stale 
revisions return a 409 conflict with current revision and authoritative state.
   - Idempotency with random `operation_id`: exact retries replay identical 
historical results without duplicate revision bumps, duplicate database writes, 
or duplicate audit events. Reusing an operation identifier with different 
parameters returns a 409 conflict.

4. **Assessment Authoring & Availability (`/app/src/routes.js`)**:
   - Instructor can author drafts with UTC opening/due dates, positive 
durations, and max attempts.
   - Multiple-choice questions validate distinct, trimmed options and matching 
answer keys. Written questions validate positive rubric criteria.
   - Publishing requires at least one question and is strictly terminal (draft 
cannot be published twice or accept questions after publish).
   - Students see only published assessments in their enrolled course and never 
receive stored answer keys.

5. **Timed Attempts & Student Accommodations (`/app/src/routes.js`)**:
   - Timing calculations incorporate student accommodations 
(`extra_time_minutes` added to duration, `deadline_extension_minutes` added to 
due date).
   - Fixed reference moment `2026-09-02T12:00:00Z` is used for all timestamping,
availability, and expiry checks.
   - Expiry occurs at `min(started_at + adjusted_duration, adjusted_due)`. 
At/after expiry, attempts accept no answer writes and auto-submit once.
   - Students can save in-progress answers. Submission calculates 
multiple-choice objective scores from the server key and preserves written 
answers.
   - Student Privacy: Prior to release, students cannot view scores, rubric 
grades, or feedback in attempts, audit logs, or responses.

6. **Single-Row & Atomic Worksheet Grading (`/app/src/routes.js`)**:
   - Instructor can grade any attempt; TA can only grade assigned attempts 
(`user_2`).
   - Atomic grading worksheet allows selecting multiple rubric rows across 
attempts, editing scores and feedback, and saving in a single atomic transaction
with one revision increment and one audit event per attempt.

7. **Reviewed Batch Release (`/app/src/routes.js`)**:
   - Two-phase release: Instructor creates a server-bound release preview (`POST
/api/release/preview`), then commits (`POST /api/release/commit`).
   - Checks revision freshness between preview and commit.
   - Atomically releases all selected attempts, bumping revision once and 
writing one release audit event per attempt. Released grades are immutable.

8. **Weighted Outcome Ledger (`/app/src/outcomes.js`)**:
   - Calculates weighted student percentages based on policy weights and maximum
points.
   - Missing and unreleased work are never treated as zero; final grades remain 
pending if positive-weight work is unreleased.
   - Excusing student assessments with a required reason removes their weight 
from the denominator and enables calculation of remaining released work.
   - Instructor can edit the entire weighting policy (must total 100.00%) or 
manage student exceptions. Students see only their own row. TA access is 
forbidden (403).

9. **Durable Audit Log (`/app/src/routes.js`)**:
   - Persists all course actions newest first.
   - Role-filtered: Instructor sees course events, TA sees assigned/own events, 
Students see own attempt events without leaking unreleased scores.

10. **Accessible Frontend & Verification (`/app/public/index.html`, 
`/app/public/style.css`, `/app/public/app.js`, `/app/APP_MANIFEST.md`, 
`/app/test/e2e.test.js`)**:
    - Accessible HTML5 SPA with landmark navigation, ARIA attributes 
(`aria-current="page"`, `aria-modal="true"`), visible focus states, focus trap 
in modals, and `prefers-reduced-motion` compliance.
    - Fully responsive from 375px mobile viewport to desktop without horizontal 
scrolling and with >= 44px touch targets.
    - Quick-select demo sign-in buttons for all four seed accounts with password
hint.
    - Comprehensive E2E test suite in `npm test` verifying all contract rules 
and requirements.
